      if (isCanonical && isMultiPart) {
        return { accepted:true, confidence:99, matched:term, reason:`정확한 물품명 “${term}”이 최근 RP에 직접 등장` };
      }

      // ② 제목 자체가 하나의 충분히 구체적인 고유 단어인 경우만 단독 허용.
      if (isCanonical && !isWeakSingle && titleSupportCount === 0) {
        return { accepted:true, confidence:97, matched:term, reason:`구체적인 물품명 “${term}”이 최근 RP에 직접 등장` };
      }

      // ③ 별칭 또는 짧은 일반명사는 같은 단어 하나만으로 선택하지 않습니다.
      //    제목에서 뽑은 소유자·색상·특징 등 다른 단서가 근처에 함께 있어야 같은 물품으로 봅니다.
      const supports = importantItemTitleSupportTerms(item, term);
      const support = importantItemSupportNearMatch(haystack, term, supports);
      if (support) {
        return {
          accepted:true, confidence:isCanonical ? 96 : 93, matched:term,
          reason:`“${term}” + 보조 단서 “${support}”가 같은 최근 RP 문맥에 함께 등장`
        };
      }

      // 제목이 단일 고유명 하나뿐이고 사용자가 같은 별칭을 직접 등록한 경우는 허용합니다.
      if (!isCanonical && !isWeakSingle && titleSupportCount === 0) {
        return { accepted:true, confidence:92, matched:term, reason:`구체적인 별칭 “${term}”이 최근 RP에 직접 등장` };
      }
    }
    return { accepted:false, confidence:0, matched:'', reason:'같은 단어만 있거나 같은 물품임을 확인할 보조 단서가 부족함' };
  }

  function parseImportantItemBulkText(text) {
    // v0.14.8:
    // 들여쓰기 없는 Markdown 불릿만 새 '상위 중요 물품' 카드로 분리합니다.
    //   - **물품 이름 | 날짜**        -> 새 카드
    //     - **원본 | NPC_D 휴대폰**    -> 직전 카드의 하위 항목
    //     - **전송본 | NPC_A 휴대폰**  -> 직전 카드의 하위 항목
    //
    // 전체 붙여넣기가 코드블록/인용 등으로 통째로 들여써진 경우에는
    // 공통 들여쓰기만 제거한 뒤 상대적인 깊이를 보존합니다.
    const rawLines = normalizeLineBreaks(String(text || ''))
      .split('\n')
      .map(line => line.replace(/^\t+/, tabs => '  '.repeat(tabs.length)));

    const nonBlank = rawLines.filter(line => line.trim());
    const commonIndent = nonBlank.length
      ? Math.min(...nonBlank.map(line => (line.match(/^ */)?.[0].length || 0)))
      : 0;

    const lines = commonIndent > 0
      ? rawLines.map(line => {
          if (!line.trim()) return line;
          const leading = line.match(/^ */)?.[0].length || 0;
          return line.slice(Math.min(commonIndent, leading));
        })
      : rawLines;

    const items = [];
    let current = null;

    const finish = () => {
      if (!current) return;

      // 부모 제목은 카드 헤더로 빠지므로 본문 전체를 Markdown 한 단계만큼
      // 들여쓰기 해제합니다. 하위 항목과 그 설명의 상대 깊이는 그대로 유지됩니다.
      current.content = current.lines
        .join('\n')
        .replace(/^(?: {2}|\t)/gm, '')
        .trim();

      delete current.lines;
      if (current.title && current.content) items.push(current);
      current = null;
    };

    for (const line of lines) {
      // 핵심: 반드시 0-depth에서 시작하는 불릿만 새 부모 카드입니다.
      // ^\s* 를 사용하면 하위 불릿까지 새 물품으로 잘못 분리되므로 금지합니다.
      const heading = line.match(/^[-*]\s+\*\*(.+?)\*\*\s*(.*)$/);
      if (heading) {
        finish();
        current = { title:cleanImportantItemTitle(heading[1]), lines:[] };
        if (heading[2]?.trim()) current.lines.push(heading[2].trim());
        continue;
      }

      // 들여쓰기된 - **...** 는 여기로 들어와 직전 부모 물품의 content에 유지됩니다.
      if (current) current.lines.push(line);
    }

    finish();
    return items;
  }

  // The stored item body remains a single raw string. Read cards are only a view of it.
  // Exact offsets let editing one child leave every other line, including nonstandard text, intact.
  function parseImportantItemReadBody(value) {
    const raw = String(value || '');
    const children = [];
    let offset = 0;
    for (const line of raw.split('\n')) {
      const heading = line.match(/^([ \t]*[-*]\s+\*\*)(.+?)(\*\*)([ \t]*.*)$/);
      if (heading) {
        if (children.length) children[children.length - 1].end = offset;
        const label = heading[2];
        const parts = label.match(/^(.*?)\s*([|｜])\s*(.*?)$/);
        children.push({ start:offset, end:raw.length, headerEnd:offset + line.length,
          prefix:heading[1], suffix:heading[4],
          name:parts ? parts[1].trim() : label.trim(),
          keeper:parts ? parts[3].trim() : '', separator:parts ? parts[2] : '' });
      }
      offset += line.length + 1;
    }
    return { raw, intro:raw.slice(0, children[0]?.start ?? raw.length), children };
  }

  function importantItemVisibleText(value) {
    return String(value || '').replace(/^\n/, '').replace(/^ {2}/gm, '').trim();
  }

  function importantItemHeaderParts(title) {
    const clean = cleanImportantItemTitle(title);
    const divider = clean.lastIndexOf('|') > clean.lastIndexOf('｜') ? clean.lastIndexOf('|') : clean.lastIndexOf('｜');
    return divider < 0
      ? { name:clean || '중요 물품', date:'' }
      : { name:clean.slice(0, divider).trim() || '중요 물품', date:clean.slice(divider + 1).trim() };
  }

  function renderImportantItemReadBody(value) {
    const parsed = parseImportantItemReadBody(value);
    const intro = importantItemVisibleText(parsed.intro);
    return `${intro ? `<div class="rpcm-item-parent-label">물품 설명</div><pre class="rpcm-item-readable">${esc(intro)}</pre>` : ''}
      ${parsed.children.length ? `<div class="rpcm-item-parent-label">하위 사진·소지품 · ${parsed.children.length}개</div>` : ''}
      ${parsed.children.map((child, index) => {
        const body = parsed.raw.slice(child.headerEnd, child.end);
        const text = importantItemVisibleText((child.suffix || '') + body);
        return `<section class="rpcm-item-child" data-item-child="${index}">
          <div class="rpcm-item-child-head"><strong>${esc(child.name || '이름 미기록')}</strong>${child.keeper ? `<span>보관자 · ${esc(child.keeper)}</span>` : ''}<button type="button" class="rpcm-item-child-edit" data-item-child-edit="${index}">편집</button></div>
          ${text ? `<pre class="rpcm-item-readable">${esc(text)}</pre>` : ''}
        </section>`;
      }).join('')}
      ${!intro && !parsed.children.length ? `<pre class="rpcm-item-readable">${esc(String(value || '').trim() || '저장된 설명이 없습니다.')}</pre>` : ''}`;
  }

  function editImportantItemChild(value, index, name, keeper, description) {
    const parsed = parseImportantItemReadBody(value);
    const child = parsed.children[index];
    if (!child || !String(name || '').trim()) return null;
    const newName = String(name).trim();
    const newKeeper = String(keeper || '').trim();
    const oldBody = parsed.raw.slice(child.headerEnd, child.end);
    const oldDescription = importantItemVisibleText(oldBody);
    const originalHeader = parsed.raw.slice(child.start, child.headerEnd);
    const heading = originalHeader.match(/^([ \t]*[-*]\s+\*\*)(.+?)(\*\*)([ \t]*.*)$/);
    if (!heading) return null;
    const label = newName === child.name && newKeeper === child.keeper
      ? heading[2] : newName + (newKeeper || child.separator ? ` ${child.separator || '|'} ${newKeeper}` : '');
    let body = oldBody;
    if (String(description) !== oldDescription) {
      const indent = oldBody.match(/\n([ \t]+)\S/)?.[1] ?? '  ';
      const originalEnding = oldBody.match(/\n+$/)?.[0] || (child.end < parsed.raw.length ? '\n' : '');
      const text = String(description).replace(/\n+$/, '');
      body = (text ? '\n' + text.split('\n').map(line => line ? indent + line : '').join('\n') : '') + originalEnding;
    }
    return parsed.raw.slice(0, child.start) + child.prefix + label + '**' + child.suffix
      + body + parsed.raw.slice(child.end);
  }

  function openImportantItemBulkImportDialog() {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      document.querySelector('#rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop')?.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop';
      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog rpcm-item-import-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="중요 물품 목록 붙여넣기">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">📦 중요 물품 목록 붙여넣기</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc"><code>- **물품 이름 | 날짜**</code>처럼 <code>|</code>와 <code>｜</code>를 모두 인식하며, <strong>들여쓰기 없는 불릿만 새 물품</strong>으로 나눕니다. <code>  - **사진/소지품 | 소유자**</code>처럼 들여쓴 불릿은 직전 물품 안에 유지됩니다. 같은 이름과 최초 날짜의 기존 카드는 새 전체 목록의 내용으로 갱신합니다. 입력에 없는 기존 카드는 유지하며, 삭제는 물품 탭의 선택 삭제를 사용하세요.</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-item-import-body"><textarea id="rpcm-item-import-text" spellcheck="false" placeholder="- **물품 이름 | 날짜**&#10;  물품의 내용과 보관 위치...&#10;  - **사진 또는 중요 소지품 | 소유자**&#10;    소유·공개 범위·정보격차...&#10;&#10;- **다음 물품 | 날짜**&#10;  다음 물품 설명..."></textarea><div id="rpcm-item-import-preview">붙여넣으면 상위 물품 카드 수를 표시합니다. 들여쓴 하위 항목은 부모 카드 안에 유지됩니다.</div></div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">물품 목록 반영</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
      const textarea = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-item-import-text');
      const preview = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-item-import-preview');
      const close = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      const refresh = () => {
        const parsed = parseImportantItemBulkText(textarea?.value || '');
        preview.textContent = parsed.length ? `${parsed.length}개 상위 물품 인식 · ${parsed.map(item => item.title).join(' · ')} · 들여쓴 하위 항목은 부모 카드 안에 유지` : '인식된 상위 물품 없음 · 새 물품 제목은 들여쓰기 없이 - **물품 이름 | 날짜** 형식으로 적어주세요.';
      };
      textarea?.addEventListener('input', refresh);
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => close(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => close(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const parsed = parseImportantItemBulkText(textarea?.value || '');
        if (!parsed.length) { notify('인식된 중요 물품이 없습니다. 새 물품은 들여쓰기 없이 - **물품 이름 | 날짜** 형식으로 적어주세요.', 'warn', 4500); return; }
        close(parsed);
      };
      backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(null); };
      backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') close(null); };
      setTimeout(() => textarea?.focus(), 0);
    });
  }

  function openImportantItemDuplicateResolverDialog(room) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const groups = duplicateImportantItemGroups(room);
      if (!groups.length) { resolve(null); return; }
      document.querySelector('#rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop')?.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop';
      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog rpcm-item-duplicate-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="중복 물품 정리">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">⚠️ 중복 물품 ${groups.length}개 그룹</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">각 그룹마다 전체를 따로 보관하거나, 남길 항목 하나를 선택하세요. 날짜·소유자·보관 위치가 다른 동명 물품은 둘 다 유지해도 됩니다.</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-item-duplicate-body">${groups.map((group, groupIndex) => `
            <section class="rpcm-item-duplicate-group" data-item-duplicate-group="${groupIndex}">
              <h3>📦 ${esc(cleanImportantItemTitle(group.items[0]?.title).split(/[|｜]/)[0] || '이름 없는 물품')} <span>${group.items.length}개</span></h3>
              <label class="keep-all"><input type="radio" name="rpcm-item-duplicate-${groupIndex}" value="keep-all" checked><span><strong>둘 다 / 전체 유지</strong><small>서로 다른 날짜나 소유 상태로 보관</small></span></label>
              ${group.items.map((item, index) => `<label><input type="radio" name="rpcm-item-duplicate-${groupIndex}" value="${esc(item.id)}"><span><strong>${index + 1}번만 남기기 · ${esc(item.title || '제목 없음')}</strong><small>${esc(item.itemFolder || '미분류')} · ${esc(String(item.content || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 180) || '내용 없음')}</small></span></label>`).join('')}
            </section>`).join('')}</div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">나중에</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">선택한 대로 정리</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
      const close = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => close(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => close(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const decisions = groups.map((group, index) => ({
          key:group.key,
          keepId:String(backdrop.querySelector(`input[name="rpcm-item-duplicate-${index}"]:checked`)?.value || 'keep-all'),
          ids:group.items.map(item => String(item.id)),
        }));
        close(decisions);
      };
      backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(null); };
      backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') close(null); };
    });
  }

  function applyImportantItemDuplicateDecisions(room, decisions) {
    if (!Array.isArray(decisions)) return 0;
    const removeIds = new Set();
    const allowed = new Set(Array.isArray(room.allowedDuplicateItemSignatures) ? room.allowedDuplicateItemSignatures.map(String) : []);
    for (const decision of decisions) {
      if (!decision) continue;
      if (decision.keepId === 'keep-all') {
        allowed.add(importantItemDuplicateSignature(decision.key, (decision.ids || []).map(id => ({ id }))));
        continue;
      }
      for (const id of decision.ids || []) if (String(id) !== String(decision.keepId)) removeIds.add(String(id));
    }
    const before = room.slots.length;
    if (removeIds.size) room.slots = room.slots.filter(slot => !removeIds.has(String(slot.id)));
    const currentSignatures = new Set(duplicateImportantItemGroups(room, { includeAllowed:true }).map(group => group.signature));
    room.allowedDuplicateItemSignatures = [...allowed].filter(signature => currentSignatures.has(signature));
    return before - room.slots.length;
  }

  const LOG_STOPWORDS = new Set([
    '그리고','하지만','그래서','그러나','그런데','그러면서','그러자','따라서','또한','혹은','또는',
    '지금','현재','오늘','어제','내일','그때','이때','이제','여전히','계속','아직','벌써','다시','이미','처음','마지막',
    '정도','때문','대한','관련','사실','상태','장면','내용','모습','순간','마음','생각','행동','말함','확정','미확정',
    '자신','자기','서로','그것','이것','저것','여기','거기','누구','무엇','어디','우리','너희','그들','그녀','그는','네가',
    '하는','했다','한다','하고','하며','하자','하면','해서','하다','해도','했고','된다','되어','돼서','되며','되다',
    '있는','있던','있다','없다','없는','없던','아니다','않다','않는','않았다','말했다','말하며','말하다','말해',
    '보며','보자','보았다','보다','믿어','믿으며','믿었다','그냥','정말','너무','매우','조금','바로','함께','혼자','같이',
    '직접','최신','자동','금지','유지','사용자','캐릭터','user','assistant'
  ]);

  // 한국어는 공백만으로 나누면 `인물의`, `조직은`처럼 조사가 붙은 표면형과
  // `그는`, `자신이` 같은 기능어가 핵심 검색어로 남습니다. 별도 형태소 분석기 없이도
  // 안전하게 구분되는 조사만 떼고, 어간을 임의 추측해야 하는 동사·형용사는 손대지 않습니다.
  const LOG_RECALL_PARTICLE_RE = /(?:으로부터|에게서는|한테서는|께서는|에서부터|에게서|한테서|으로서는|으로써는|이라도|이라면|이라고|이라는|이랑은|하고는|에서는|으로는|로부터|까지는|부터는|보다는|처럼은|만으로|만이라도|에게|한테|께서|에서|으로|로서|로써|부터|까지|보다|처럼|만큼|이랑|하고|이라|이며|이고|이나|라도|마저|조차|밖에|대로|은|는|이|가|을|를|의|에|도|만|와|과|랑)$/;

  function normalizeRecallToken(value) {
    let token = String(value || '').toLowerCase().replace(/^[-_']+|[-_']+$/g, '');
    if (!token || /^\d+$/.test(token)) return '';
    // `여전히`처럼 마지막 음절이 우연히 조사와 같은 부사는 먼저 제외합니다.
    if (LOG_STOPWORDS.has(token)) return '';
    if (/^[가-힣]+$/.test(token)) {
      // 한 번에 가장 긴 조사 하나만 제거합니다. 두 글자 이상의 본체를 반드시 남겨
      // 한 글자 단어나 실제 이름을 과도하게 훼손하지 않습니다.
      const stripped = token.replace(LOG_RECALL_PARTICLE_RE, '');
      if (stripped.length >= 2) token = stripped;
    }
    if (token.length < 2 || LOG_STOPWORDS.has(token)) return '';
    return token;
  }

  function tokenizeRecallText(text) {
    const raw = String(text || '').toLowerCase().match(/[\p{L}\p{N}_'-]{2,}/gu) || [];
    return [...new Set(raw.map(normalizeRecallToken).filter(Boolean))];
  }

  function simpleHash(value) {
    let h = 2166136261;
    const str = String(value || '');
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
      h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
      h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
    }
    return (h >>> 0).toString(36);
  }

  const DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE = '기본 시간선';
  const DEFAULT_REGRESSION_TIMELINE = '회귀 시간선';

  // 날짜가 과거로 돌아간 것만으로는 회상·과거편·오기와 시간회귀를 구분할 수 없습니다.
  // 같은 날짜 블록이 겹친 상황에서 아래처럼 명시적인 분기 신호가 있을 때만
  // "시간선 분기 가능성"을 제안하고, 실제 분리는 사용자가 확인하도록 합니다.
  const LOG_TIMELINE_BRANCH_HINT_RE = /(시간\s*회귀|타임\s*(?:리프|루프|슬립)|과거(?:로|에)\s*(?:돌아(?:가|왔|온|감)|회귀)|(?:\d+|[일이삼사오육칠팔구십백]+)\s*(?:일|주|개월|년)\s*전(?:으로|의)\s*(?:돌아|회귀)|회귀\s*(?:전|후|했|한|하여|했다|현재\s*시간선)|미래(?:의)?\s*기억|이전\s*생|전생\s*기억|평행\s*(?:세계|우주)|분기\s*시간선|원래\s*시간선|(?:IF|what[- ]?if)\s*(?:분기|루트|세계선))/i;

  function timelineBranchHint(text) {
    const match = String(text || '').match(LOG_TIMELINE_BRANCH_HINT_RE);
    return match ? String(match[0] || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : '';
  }

  function timelineBranchHintForGroup(group) {
    if (!group?.blocks?.length) return '';
    return timelineBranchHint(group.blocks.map(block => `${block.heading}\n${block.body}`).join('\n'));
  }

  function sanitizeLogTimelineLabel(value) {
    return String(value || '').replace(/[\[\]\r\n|｜]/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 40);
  }

  function cleanLogTimelineLabel(value) {
    const label = sanitizeLogTimelineLabel(value);
    if (/^(?:기본|원래|원본|본래)\s*시간선$/i.test(label)) return DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
    if (/^(?:회귀|회귀\s*후)\s*시간선$/i.test(label)) return DEFAULT_REGRESSION_TIMELINE;
    return label;
  }

  function normalizedLogTimelineKey(value) {
    const label = cleanLogTimelineLabel(value);
    return !label || label === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '__main__' : label.toLocaleLowerCase();
  }

  function logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block) {
    return cleanLogTimelineLabel(block?.timelineLabel) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
  }

  function normalizeRoomLogTimelines(room) {
    if (!room || typeof room !== 'object') return [DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE];
    const raw = Array.isArray(room.logTimelines) ? room.logTimelines : [];
    const labels = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    const add = value => {
      const label = cleanLogTimelineLabel(value) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
      const key = normalizedLogTimelineKey(label === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : label);
      if (seen.has(key)) return;
      seen.add(key);
      labels.push(label);
    };
    add(DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE);
    raw.forEach(add);
    const log = (room.slots || []).find(slot => slot.id === 'logSummary');
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(String(log?.content || ''));
    blocks.forEach(block => add(logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block)));
    const legacyKeyMap = new Map();
    blocks.forEach(block => {
      if (block.isUnknown || !block.sourceTimelineLabel) return;
      const legacyTimelineKey = String(block.sourceTimelineLabel).toLocaleLowerCase();
      if (legacyTimelineKey === block.timelineKey) return;
      const legacyDateKey = `${block.calendarDateKey}::${legacyTimelineKey}`;
      legacyKeyMap.set(`${legacyDateKey}-${simpleHash(block.heading)}`, String(block.key));
    });
    if (legacyKeyMap.size) remapLogSelectionKeys(room, legacyKeyMap, blocks);
    room.logTimelines = labels;
    const requested = cleanLogTimelineLabel(room.activeLogTimeline);
    room.activeLogTimeline = labels.find(label => normalizedLogTimelineKey(label === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : label) === normalizedLogTimelineKey(requested === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : requested)) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
    return labels;
  }

  function nextLogTimelineRoundLabel(labels) {
    const used = new Set((labels || []).map(label => normalizedLogTimelineKey(label)));
    if (!used.has(normalizedLogTimelineKey(DEFAULT_REGRESSION_TIMELINE))) return DEFAULT_REGRESSION_TIMELINE;
    let round = 1;
    while (used.has(normalizedLogTimelineKey(`${round}회차`))) round++;
    return `${round}회차`;
  }

  function activeLogTimelineLabel(room) {
    normalizeRoomLogTimelines(room);
    return cleanLogTimelineLabel(room?.activeLogTimeline) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
  }

  function makeStoryCardId() {
    return `TL_${Date.now().toString(36)}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`;
  }

  function normalizeStoryDate(value) {
    const raw = String(value || '').trim().replace(/[./]/g, '-');
    const match = raw.match(/^(\d{1,6})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})$/);
    if (!match) return '';
    return `${match[1].padStart(4, '0')}-${match[2].padStart(2, '0')}-${match[3].padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }

  function normalizeStoryTimelineCards(room) {
    if (!room || typeof room !== 'object') return [];
    const labels = normalizeRoomLogTimelines(room);
    const labelByKey = new Map(labels.map(label => [normalizedLogTimelineKey(label), label]));
    const fallbackLabel = activeLogTimelineLabel(room);
    const seenIds = new Set();
    const now = nowIso();
    room.storyTimelineCards = (Array.isArray(room.storyTimelineCards) ? room.storyTimelineCards : []).map((raw, index) => {
      const source = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {};
      let cardId = String(source.cardId || '').trim();
      if (!/^TL_[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(cardId) || seenIds.has(cardId)) cardId = makeStoryCardId();
      seenIds.add(cardId);
      const requestedLabel = cleanLogTimelineLabel(source.timelineLabel) || fallbackLabel;
      // timelineLabel은 별도 타임라인 문서가 아니라 카드의 출처 세계선 표시에만 사용합니다.
      const timelineLabel = labelByKey.get(normalizedLogTimelineKey(requestedLabel)) || requestedLabel || fallbackLabel;
      const tags = Array.isArray(source.tags)
        ? source.tags.map(tag => String(tag || '').trim()).filter(Boolean)
        : String(source.tags || '').split(/[,，#]/).map(tag => tag.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        cardId,
        timelineLabel,
        dateStart:normalizeStoryDate(source.dateStart),
        dateEnd:normalizeStoryDate(source.dateEnd),
        title:String(source.title || '').trim(),
        content:String(source.content || '').trim(),
        tags:[...new Set(tags)],
        userLocked:!!source.userLocked,
        inject:source.inject !== false,
        sortOrder:Number.isFinite(Number(source.sortOrder)) ? Number(source.sortOrder) : index,
        createdAt:String(source.createdAt || now),
        updatedAt:String(source.updatedAt || source.createdAt || now),
      };
    });
    return room.storyTimelineCards;
  }

  function storyDateSortValue(value) {
    const normalized = normalizeStoryDate(value);
    if (!normalized) return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
    const parts = normalized.split('-').map(Number);
    return parts[0] * 10000 + parts[1] * 100 + parts[2];
  }

  function sortedStoryTimelineCards(cards) {
    return [...(cards || [])].sort((a, b) => {
      const date = storyDateSortValue(a.dateStart) - storyDateSortValue(b.dateStart);
      if (date) return date;
      const end = storyDateSortValue(a.dateEnd || a.dateStart) - storyDateSortValue(b.dateEnd || b.dateStart);
      if (end) return end;
      return Number(a.sortOrder || 0) - Number(b.sortOrder || 0);
    });
  }

  function orderedStoryTimelineCards(cards) {
    return [...(cards || [])].sort((a, b) => Number(a.sortOrder || 0) - Number(b.sortOrder || 0));
  }

  function storyTimelineCardsFor(room) {
    return orderedStoryTimelineCards(normalizeStoryTimelineCards(room));
  }

  function storyCardDateLabel(card) {
    const start = String(card?.dateStart || '').replace(/-/g, '.');
    const end = String(card?.dateEnd || '').replace(/-/g, '.');
    if (!start) return '날짜 미정';
    return end && end !== start ? `${start} ~ ${end}` : start;
  }

  function storyTimelineSummary(room) {
    const cards = normalizeStoryTimelineCards(room);
    const enabled = room?.storyTimelineInjectionEnabled !== false;
    const eligible = cards.filter(card => card.inject && (card.title || card.content));
    const chars = enabled ? eligible.reduce((sum, card) => sum + String(card.title || '').length + String(card.content || '').length + (card.tags || []).join(',').length, 0) : 0;
    return { count:cards.length, injectCount:enabled ? eligible.length : 0, eligibleCount:eligible.length, chars, enabled };
  }

  // 외부 [인물정보] 방의 결과물은 정해진 JSONL 형식만 해석합니다. RP 원문 자체는 추측해 분류하지 않습니다.
  function normalizePersonStateRecords(room) {
    if (!room || typeof room !== 'object') return [];
    const seen = new Set();
    room.personStateRecords = (Array.isArray(room.personStateRecords) ? room.personStateRecords : [])
      .filter(record => record && ['speech','relationship'].includes(record.type)).map(record => {
        record.id = String(record.id || 'ps_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9));
        while (seen.has(record.id)) record.id += '_';
        seen.add(record.id);
        for (const field of ['key','person','target','address','tone','context','official','stance','conflict','evidence']) record[field] = String(record[field] || '');
        if (record.type === 'relationship') record.trajectory = String(record.trajectory || '');
        record.active = record.active !== false;
        record.inject = record.inject !== false;
        return record;
      });

    const speechSeen = new Map();
    room.personStateRecords = room.personStateRecords.filter(record => {
      if (record.type !== 'speech' || !record.active) return true;
      const norm = value => String(value || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR').replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
      const signature = [record.person, record.target, record.address, record.tone, record.context].map(norm).join('\u0000');
      if (!signature.replace(/\u0000/g, '')) return true;
      const existing = speechSeen.get(signature);
      if (!existing) {
        speechSeen.set(signature, record);
        return true;
      }
      existing.inject = existing.inject || record.inject;
      if (!String(existing.key || '').trim() && String(record.key || '').trim()) existing.key = record.key;
      if (String(record.evidence || '').trim().length > String(existing.evidence || '').trim().length) existing.evidence = record.evidence;
      if (!existing.updatedAt && record.updatedAt) existing.updatedAt = record.updatedAt;
      return false;
    });

    room.personStateInjectionEnabled = room.personStateInjectionEnabled === true;
    return room.personStateRecords;
  }

  function parsePersonStateOutput(text) {
    const src = String(text || '').replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').trim();
    const lines = src.split('\n');
    const errors = [];
    if (lines[0]?.trim() !== '[[WISH_PERSON_V1]]' || lines.at(-1)?.trim() !== '[[/WISH_PERSON_V1]]') {
      return {records:[], errors:['첫 줄 [[WISH_PERSON_V1]]과 마지막 줄 [[/WISH_PERSON_V1]]을 확인해 주세요.']};
    }
    if (src.length > 600000 || lines.length > 2200) return {records:[], errors:['한 번에 가져올 수 있는 결과물 크기를 초과했습니다. 결과를 나누어 주세요.']};
    const records = [];
    const used = new Set();
    for (let i = 1; i < lines.length - 1; i++) {
      const line = lines[i].trim();
      if (!line) continue;
      let raw;
      try { raw = JSON.parse(line); } catch (_) { errors.push(`${i + 1}행: 한 줄짜리 JSON 객체가 아닙니다.`); continue; }
      if (!raw || Array.isArray(raw) || typeof raw !== 'object') { errors.push(`${i + 1}행: 객체가 아닙니다.`); continue; }
      const type = String(raw.type || ''), op = String(raw.op || '');
      const person = String(raw.person || '').trim(), target = String(raw.target || '').trim();
      const key = String(raw.key || '').trim(), id = String(raw.id || '').trim();
      const evidence = String(raw.evidence || '').trim();
      if (!['knowledge','speech','relationship'].includes(type) || !['upsert','end'].includes(op) || !person || (!id && !/^[a-z0-9_]{2,90}$/.test(key)) || !evidence) {
        errors.push(`${i + 1}행: type/op/person/key(또는 id)/evidence를 확인해 주세요.`); continue;
      }
      if (type !== 'knowledge' && !target) { errors.push(`${i + 1}행: 상대 인물이 없습니다.`); continue; }
      if(type==='relationship'&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw,'graph')&&raw.graph!==null&&(!raw.graph||typeof raw.graph!=='object'||Array.isArray(raw.graph))){errors.push(`${i+1}행: 관계도 구조 graph는 객체 또는 null이어야 합니다.`);continue;}
      if (type === 'relationship' && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw, 'trajectory') && typeof raw.trajectory !== 'string') {
        errors.push(`${i + 1}행: 핵심 전환은 문자열이어야 합니다.`); continue;
      }
      const record = {type,op,id,key,person,target,evidence,
        status:String(raw.status || ''),fact:String(raw.fact || '').trim(),actualFact:String(raw.actualFact || '').trim(),
        hiddenFrom:Array.isArray(raw.hiddenFrom)?[...new Set(raw.hiddenFrom.map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))]:[],
        concealmentNote:String(raw.concealmentNote || '').trim(),
        concealments:Array.isArray(raw.concealments)?raw.concealments.filter(row=>row&&typeof row==='object').map(row=>({holder:String(row.holder||'').trim(),target:String(row.target||'').trim(),scope:String(row.scope||'').trim(),publicVersion:String(row.publicVersion||'').trim()})).filter(row=>row.holder&&row.target):[],
        hiddenFromPresent:Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw,'hiddenFrom'),concealmentNotePresent:Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw,'concealmentNote'),concealmentsPresent:Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw,'concealments'),
        address:String(raw.address || '').trim(),tone:String(raw.tone || '').trim(),context:String(raw.context || '').trim(),
        official:String(raw.official || '').trim(),stance:String(raw.stance || '').trim(),conflict:String(raw.conflict || '').trim(),
        trajectory:String(raw.trajectory || '').trim(), trajectoryPresent:Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw,'trajectory'),graph:normalizeRelationshipGraph(raw.graph,person,target),graphPresent:Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw,'graph')};
      if (type === 'relationship' && record.trajectory.length > 1200) { errors.push(`${i + 1}행: 핵심 전환은 1,200자를 넘을 수 없습니다.`); continue; }
      if (op === 'upsert' && ((type === 'knowledge' && (!KNOWLEDGE_STATUSES.includes(record.status) || !record.fact || (record.status === 'mistaken' && !record.actualFact)))
        || (type === 'speech' && !record.address && !record.tone)
        || (type === 'relationship' && !record.official && !record.stance && !record.conflict))) {
        errors.push(`${i + 1}행: ${type}의 필수 현재값이 없습니다.`); continue;
      }
      const identity = `${type}\u0000${person.toLocaleLowerCase()}\u0000${target.toLocaleLowerCase()}\u0000${id || key}`;
      if (used.has(identity)) { errors.push(`${i + 1}행: 같은 카드가 결과물에서 중복됐습니다.`); continue; }
      used.add(identity);
      records.push(record);
    }
    return {records,errors};
  }

  function personRecordMatch(room, entry) {
    const source = entry.type === 'knowledge' ? normalizeKnowledgeCards(room) : normalizePersonStateRecords(room);
    const candidates = source.filter(record => entry.type === 'knowledge' || record.type === entry.type);
    if (entry.id) {
      const idMatch = candidates.filter(record => record.id === entry.id);
      if (idMatch.length === 1) return idMatch[0];
    }
    const samePerson = value => String(value || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
    const same = candidates.filter(record => samePerson(record.person) === samePerson(entry.person)
      && (entry.type === 'knowledge' || samePerson(record.target) === samePerson(entry.target))
      && ((entry.key && String(record.key || '') === entry.key)
        || (entry.type === 'knowledge' && entry.fact && samePerson(record.fact) === samePerson(entry.fact))));
    if (same.length === 1) return same[0];

    if (entry.type === 'speech') {
      const speechSame = candidates.filter(record => record.type === 'speech'
        && samePerson(record.person) === samePerson(entry.person)
        && samePerson(record.target) === samePerson(entry.target)
        && samePerson(record.address) === samePerson(entry.address)
        && samePerson(record.tone) === samePerson(entry.tone)
        && samePerson(record.context) === samePerson(entry.context));
      if (speechSame.length === 1) return speechSame[0];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function personStateExportText(room) {
    const lines = ['[[WISH_PERSON_V1]]'];
    for (const card of normalizeKnowledgeCards(room).filter(card => !card.archived)) lines.push(JSON.stringify({type:'knowledge',id:card.id,key:card.key || '',person:card.person,status:card.status,fact:card.fact,actualFact:card.actualFact || '',evidence:card.evidence || '',hiddenFrom:card.hiddenFrom||[],concealmentNote:card.concealmentNote||'',concealments:card.concealments||[],inject:!!card.inject}));
    for (const record of normalizePersonStateRecords(room).filter(record => record.active)) lines.push(JSON.stringify({type:record.type,id:record.id,key:record.key,person:record.person,target:record.target,address:record.address,tone:record.tone,context:record.context,official:record.official,stance:record.stance,conflict:record.conflict,...(record.type === 'relationship' ? {trajectory:record.trajectory || '',...(record.graph?{graph:normalizeRelationshipGraph(record.graph,record.person,record.target)}:{})} : {}),evidence:record.evidence,inject:record.inject}));
    lines.push('[[/WISH_PERSON_V1]]');
    return lines.join('\n');
  }

  // 인지 카드는 현재상태에서 자동 추출하지 않습니다. 명시적으로 확인한 내용만 보관합니다.
  const KNOWLEDGE_STATUSES = ['knows', 'does-not-know', 'suspects', 'mistaken', 'unconfirmed'];
  const KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_DEFS = Object.freeze([
    { id:'relationship', label:'관계·감정' },
    { id:'secret', label:'비밀·정보' },
    { id:'item', label:'물품·소지' },
    { id:'event', label:'사건·과거' },
    { id:'place', label:'위치·일정' },
    { id:'body', label:'신체·능력' },
    { id:'etc', label:'기타' },
  ]);
  const KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_IDS = new Set(['auto', ...KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_DEFS.map(row => row.id)]);
  function normalizeKnowledgeCategory(value) {
    const category = String(value || 'auto').trim();
    return KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_IDS.has(category) ? category : 'auto';
  }
  function inferredKnowledgeCategory(card) {
    const manual = normalizeKnowledgeCategory(card?.category);
    if (manual !== 'auto') return manual;
    const text = [card?.fact, card?.actualFact, card?.evidence, card?.key].map(value => String(value || '')).join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
    const has = pattern => pattern.test(text);
    if (has(/비밀|정체|본명|신원|정보|은폐|숨기|감추|소문|거짓말|오해|의심|private|패스워드|암호|기밀|공개\s*여부/)) return 'secret';
    if (has(/반지|목걸이|팔찌|귀걸이|사진|편지|메시지|문서|증거|열쇠|휴대폰|핸드폰|폰\b|가방|선물|소지|보관|물건|장비|도구|무기|총\b|차량|자동차|카드키|약\b/)) return 'item';
    if (has(/부상|상처|통증|치료|회복|질병|병\b|임신|신체|몸\b|혈액|생리|알레르기|약물|능력|특수능력|초능력|마법|변신|저주|후유증|기억상실/)) return 'body';
    if (has(/위치|장소|어디|집\b|숙소|기지|학교|병원|회사|도시|국가|출국|귀국|여행|일정|예약|약속\s*시간|만남\s*예정|계획|출발|도착|근무|수업|행사|파티/)) return 'place';
    if (has(/관계|연애|연인|사랑|호감|감정|질투|신뢰|불신|친구|썸\b|고백|결별|이별|헤어|화해|용서|독점|동거|약혼|결혼|배우자|성적\s*관계|키스/)) return 'relationship';
    if (has(/사건|과거|예전|첫\s|처음|폭행|사고|납치|실종|임무|작전|전투|경기|수사|범행|살인|구조|재회|만났|만남|발견|목격|공격|체포|도주/)) return 'event';
    return 'etc';
  }
  function knowledgeCategoryLabel(card) {
    const id = inferredKnowledgeCategory(card);
    return KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_DEFS.find(row => row.id === id)?.label || '기타';
  }

  function normalizeKnowledgeCards(room) {
    if (!room || typeof room !== 'object') return [];
    const entries = Array.isArray(room.knowledgeCards) ? room.knowledgeCards : [];
    const seen = new Set();
    room.knowledgeCards = entries.filter(card => card && typeof card === 'object').map(card => {
      const entry = card;
      entry.id = String(entry.id || 'kc_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9));
      while (seen.has(entry.id)) entry.id += '_';
      seen.add(entry.id);
      entry.person = String(entry.person || '');
      entry.fact = String(entry.fact || '');
      entry.status = KNOWLEDGE_STATUSES.includes(entry.status) ? entry.status : 'unconfirmed';
      entry.evidence = String(entry.evidence || '');
      entry.actualFact = String(entry.actualFact || '');
      entry.hiddenFrom = Array.isArray(entry.hiddenFrom) ? [...new Set(entry.hiddenFrom.map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))] : [];
      entry.concealmentNote = String(entry.concealmentNote || '');
      entry.key = String(entry.key || '');
      entry.category = normalizeKnowledgeCategory(entry.category);
      entry.archived = entry.archived === true;
      entry.inject = entry.inject === true;
      return entry;
    });
    // 기존 방은 주입 상태를 스스로 결정하게 합니다.
    room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled = room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled === true;
    return room.knowledgeCards;
  }

  function knowledgeConflicts(room) {
    const grouped = new Map();
    for (const card of normalizeKnowledgeCards(room)) {
      if (card.archived) continue;
      if (!card.inject || card.status === 'unconfirmed') continue;
      const key = `${card.person.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}\u0000${card.fact.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}`;
      if (!card.person.trim() || !card.fact.trim()) continue;
      if (!grouped.has(key)) grouped.set(key, new Set());
      grouped.get(key).add(card.status);
    }
    return new Set([...grouped].filter(([, statuses]) => statuses.size > 1).map(([key]) => key));
  }

  function currentPersonPlayerName(room) {
    const chosen = String(room?.personPlayerName || '').trim();
    if (chosen) return chosen;
    const records = normalizePersonStateRecords(room).filter(record => record.active && record.target.trim());
    const counts = new Map();
    for (const record of records) {
      const name = record.target.trim();
      if (name === record.person.trim()) continue;
      counts.set(name, (counts.get(name) || 0) + 1);
    }
    const best = [...counts].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
    if (best?.[1] >= 2) return best[0];
    const names = new Set(normalizeKnowledgeCards(room).filter(card => !card.archived).map(card => card.person.trim()));
    return names.has('USER') && names.size > 1 ? 'USER' : '';
  }

  function personInformationBundles(room) {
    const player = currentPersonPlayerName(room);
    const byName = new Map();
    const get = name => {
      const key = String(name || '').trim();
      if (!key || (player && key.toLocaleLowerCase() === player.toLocaleLowerCase())) return null;
      if (!byName.has(key)) byName.set(key, { name:key, knowledge:[], speech:[], relationship:[] });
      return byName.get(key);
    };
    for (const card of normalizeKnowledgeCards(room)) if (!card.archived) get(card.person)?.knowledge.push(card);
    for (const record of normalizePersonStateRecords(room)) if (record.active) get(record.person)?.[record.type]?.push(record);
    return [...byName.values()];
  }

  function personInformationItem(room, bundle) {
    const conflict = knowledgeConflicts(room);
    const name = bundle.name;
    const player = currentPersonPlayerName(room);
    const knowledge = room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled ? bundle.knowledge.filter(card => {
      const key = `${card.person.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}\u0000${card.fact.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}`;
      return card.inject && card.fact.trim() && card.status !== 'unconfirmed'
        && (card.status !== 'mistaken' || card.actualFact.trim()) && !conflict.has(key);
    }) : [];
    const speech = room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? bundle.speech.filter(record => record.inject && (!player || record.target.trim() === player)) : [];
    const relationships = room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? bundle.relationship.filter(record => record.inject && (!player || record.target.trim() === player)) : [];
    if (!knowledge.length && !speech.length && !relationships.length) return null;
    const labels = { knows:'알고 있음', 'does-not-know':'모름 (직접 확인)', suspects:'의심 중', mistaken:'오해' };
    const related = relationships.map(record => [record.official && `실제 관계: ${record.official}`, record.stance && `${name}의 태도: ${record.stance}`, record.conflict && `남은 갈등·경계: ${record.conflict}`, record.trajectory && `핵심 전환: ${record.trajectory}`].filter(Boolean).join('\n')).join('\n\n');
    const spoken = speech.map(record => [record.address && `호칭: ${record.address}`, record.tone && `말투: ${record.tone}`, record.context && `적용 조건: ${record.context}`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')).join('\n');
    const known = knowledge.map(card => `- [${labels[card.status]}] ${card.fact.trim()}${card.status === 'mistaken' ? ` (객관적 사실: ${card.actualFact.trim()})` : ''}${card.status === 'knows' && card.hiddenFrom?.length ? ` · ${name}이(가) ${card.hiddenFrom.join('·')}에게 숨김${card.concealmentNote ? ` (${card.concealmentNote})` : ''}` : ''}`).join('\n');
    const content = [
      `[${name} → ${player || '상대'} · 인물정보]`,
      '한 인물의 인지·태도·호칭은 다른 인물의 지식이나 USER의 내면으로 옮기지 않는다.',
      related && `[관계·감정선]\n${related}`,
      spoken && `[호칭·말투]\n${spoken}`,
      known && `[인지 상태]\n${known}`,
    ].filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    return { slotId:'person:' + encodeURIComponent(name), personName:name, title:`${name} · 인물정보`, group:'knowledge', content,
      totalTurns:APP.defaultRetentionTurns, usedTurns:0, autoType:'person', recallReason:`최근 RP에서 ${name} 언급 · 인지·관계·호칭 함께 선택` };
  }

  function knowledgeInjectionItems(room, contextText = '') {
    const cleanText = String(contextText || '');
    if (!cleanText.trim()) return [];
    return personInformationBundles(room).filter(bundle => {
      const slot = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.group === 'character' && String(s.title || '').trim() === bundle.name);
      const evidence = characterRpDetectionEvidence(cleanText, { title:bundle.name, aliases:Array.isArray(slot?.aliases) ? slot.aliases : [] });
      return evidence.accepted;
    }).map(bundle => personInformationItem(room, bundle)).filter(Boolean);
  }

  function isKnowledgePendingItem(item) {
    return item?.slotId === 'knowledge:master' || String(item?.slotId || '').startsWith('person:');
  }

  async function rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room) {
    if (!room?.pending) return;
    const pending = room.pending;
    ensureVerifiedPendingSnapshot(pending);
    const oldItems = clonePendingItems(pending.items);
    const oldRemoved = clonePendingItems(pending.quickRemovedItems);
    const activeNames = new Set(knowledgeInjectionItems(room, room.autoRecallContextText).map(item => item.personName));
    const bundles = new Map(personInformationBundles(room).map(bundle => [bundle.name, bundle]));
    const removedKeys = new Set(quickRemovedPendingItems(pending).map(item => item.slotId));
    const fresh = [...activeNames].map(name => bundles.get(name)).filter(Boolean).map(bundle => personInformationItem(room, bundle)).filter(Boolean);
    const previousById = new Map((pending.items || []).filter(isKnowledgePendingItem).map(item => [item.slotId, item]));
    pending.items = (pending.items || []).filter(item => !isKnowledgePendingItem(item));
    for (const item of fresh) {
      if (removedKeys.has(item.slotId)) continue;
      const previous = previousById.get(item.slotId);
      pending.items.push({ ...item, totalTurns:previous?.totalTurns ?? item.totalTurns, usedTurns:previous?.usedTurns ?? 0 });
    }
    const before = oldItems.filter(isKnowledgePendingItem).map(item => [item.slotId,item.content,item.totalTurns,item.usedTurns]);
    const after = pending.items.filter(isKnowledgePendingItem).map(item => [item.slotId,item.content,item.totalTurns,item.usedTurns]);
    if (JSON.stringify(before) === JSON.stringify(after)) return;
    try {
      return await syncPendingCarrier(room, 'person-bundles-edit');
    } catch (error) {
      if (room.pending) {
        room.pending.items = oldItems;
        room.pending.quickRemovedItems = oldRemoved;
        savePendingBackup(room.chatId, room.pending);
        await saveRoom(room);
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }

  function renderKnowledgePanel(room, overlay) {
    rpcmNewBadgeSchedule();
    const panel = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-knowledge-panel');
    if (!panel) return;
    const editingIds = new Set([...panel.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-knowledge-fields:not([hidden])')].map(node => node.closest('[data-knowledge-id]')?.dataset.knowledgeId));
    const cards = normalizeKnowledgeCards(room).filter(card => !card.archived);
    const categoryIds = new Set(['all', ...KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_DEFS.map(row => row.id)]);
    const activeCategory = categoryIds.has(state.personKnowledgeCategory) ? state.personKnowledgeCategory : 'all';
    state.personKnowledgeCategory = activeCategory;
    const visibleCards = activeCategory === 'all' ? cards : cards.filter(card => inferredKnowledgeCategory(card) === activeCategory);
    const categoryCounts = Object.fromEntries(KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_DEFS.map(row => [row.id, cards.filter(card => inferredKnowledgeCategory(card) === row.id).length]));
    const conflicts = knowledgeConflicts(room);
    const eligible = cards.filter(card => card.inject && card.person.trim() && card.fact.trim() && card.status !== 'unconfirmed' && (card.status !== 'mistaken' || card.actualFact.trim())).length;
    const actual = room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled ? eligible - cards.filter(card => {
      const key = `${card.person.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}\u0000${card.fact.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}`;
      return card.inject && conflicts.has(key);
    }).length : 0;
    const label = { knows:'알고 있음', 'does-not-know':'모름 (명시 확인)', suspects:'추측 / 의심', mistaken:'오해', unconfirmed:'미확인 (주입 제외)' };
    const categoryTabs = `<div class="rpcm-knowledge-category-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="인지 카드 카테고리"><button type="button" data-knowledge-category="all" aria-selected="${activeCategory === 'all'}">전체 <small>${cards.length}</small></button>${KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_DEFS.map(row => `<button type="button" data-knowledge-category="${row.id}" aria-selected="${activeCategory === row.id}">${row.label} <small>${categoryCounts[row.id] || 0}</small></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
    panel.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-knowledge-head"><div><strong>🔎 인물별 인지 현황 · ${cards.length}개</strong><span>인지 카드를 내용별로 자동 분류해 모아 봅니다. 카드 편집에서 분류를 직접 고정할 수도 있습니다.</span></div><label><input type="checkbox" id="rpcm-knowledge-enabled" ${room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled ? 'checked' : ''}> 인지 카드 주입 ${room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</label><button type="button" id="rpcm-knowledge-add">＋ 카드 추가</button></div>
      <div class="rpcm-knowledge-note">확인되지 않은 ‘모름’은 만들지 마세요. <b>미확인</b>은 저장만 되고 주입에서 빠집니다. 이름과 사실이 같은 카드의 판정이 충돌하면 해당 카드들을 주입하지 않습니다. ${room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled ? `현재 주입 가능 ${Math.max(0, actual)}개` : `주입 OFF · 포함 체크 ${eligible}개`}${conflicts.size ? ` · <b>충돌 ${conflicts.size}건 확인 필요</b>` : ''}</div>
      ${categoryTabs}
      <div class="rpcm-knowledge-list">${visibleCards.length ? visibleCards.map(card => {
        const key = `${card.person.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}\u0000${card.fact.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()}`;
        const category = inferredKnowledgeCategory(card);
        const categoryLabel = knowledgeCategoryLabel(card);
        const categoryOptions = `<option value="auto"${card.category === 'auto' ? ' selected' : ''}>자동 분류 · ${categoryLabel}</option>${KNOWLEDGE_CATEGORY_DEFS.map(row => `<option value="${row.id}"${card.category === row.id ? ' selected' : ''}>${row.label}</option>`).join('')}`;
        return `<details class="rpcm-knowledge-card${conflicts.has(key) && card.inject ? ' has-conflict' : ''}" data-knowledge-id="${esc(card.id)}" data-knowledge-category-value="${category}"><summary><strong>${esc(card.person.trim() || '인물 이름 입력')}</strong><span>${esc(card.fact.trim() || '인지할 사실 입력')}</span><em>${esc(label[card.status])}</em><small class="rpcm-knowledge-category-badge">${esc(categoryLabel)}</small>${card.inject ? '<small>포함</small>' : ''}</summary><div class="rpcm-knowledge-read"><div><strong>${esc(card.fact.trim() || '인지할 사실을 기록해 주세요.')}</strong><span>${esc(card.status === 'mistaken' && card.actualFact.trim() ? '실제 사실: ' + card.actualFact.trim() + ' · ' : '')}${esc(card.evidence.trim() || '확인 근거 미기록')}</span></div><button type="button" data-action="edit">편집</button></div><div class="rpcm-knowledge-fields" hidden><label>인물<input data-field="person" type="text" value="${esc(card.person)}" placeholder="예: NPC_A"></label><label>카테고리<select data-field="category">${categoryOptions}</select></label><label>인지 대상 사실<textarea data-field="fact" rows="2" placeholder="한 카드에는 하나의 사실만 기록">${esc(card.fact)}</textarea></label><label>현재 인지 상태<select data-field="status">${KNOWLEDGE_STATUSES.map(status => `<option value="${status}"${card.status === status ? ' selected' : ''}>${label[status]}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>오해의 실제 사실 · 해당 시<textarea data-field="actualFact" rows="2" placeholder="오해 카드라면 객관적으로 확인된 실제 사실">${esc(card.actualFact)}</textarea></label><label>숨기는 대상 · 알고 있음일 때만 표시<input data-field="hiddenFrom" value="${esc((card.hiddenFrom||[]).join(', '))}" placeholder="숨기는 대상 이름 · 쉼표로 구분"></label><label>은폐 내용·공개용 설명<textarea data-field="concealmentNote" rows="2" placeholder="실제로 숨긴 범위와 설명을 적으세요. 단순히 상대가 모른다는 뜻이 아닙니다.">${esc(card.concealmentNote||'')}</textarea></label><label>확인 근거<textarea data-field="evidence" rows="2" placeholder="예: 8월 1일 RP에서 직접 전달함">${esc(card.evidence)}</textarea></label><div class="rpcm-knowledge-actions"><label><input type="checkbox" data-field="inject" ${card.inject ? 'checked' : ''}> 주입에 포함</label><button type="button" data-action="delete">삭제</button></div></div></details>`;
      }).join('') : `<div class="rpcm-knowledge-empty">${cards.length ? '이 카테고리에 해당하는 인지 카드가 없습니다.' : '등록된 카드가 없습니다. 인물이 실제로 알거나 모른다고 확인된 사실부터 추가하세요.'}</div>`}</div>`;
    panel.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-knowledge-card').forEach(node => {
      if (editingIds.has(node.dataset.knowledgeId)) {
        node.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-fields').hidden = false;
        node.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-read').hidden = true;
      }
    });

    const persist = async () => {
      if (rpcmHasActiveComposition()) { setTimeout(() => void persist(), 200); return; }
      try {
        await saveRoom(room);
        await rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room);
      } catch (error) { notify(`인지 카드 주입 갱신 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6000); }
    };
    const saveTimers = new Map();
    const editCard = (field, immediate = false) => {
      const node = field.closest('[data-knowledge-id]');
      const card = cards.find(item => item.id === node?.dataset.knowledgeId);
      if (!card) return;
      if(field.dataset.field==='hiddenFrom')card.hiddenFrom=[...new Set(String(field.value||'').split(/[,，]/).map(value=>value.trim()).filter(Boolean))];
      else if(field.dataset.field==='category')card.category=normalizeKnowledgeCategory(field.value);
      else card[field.dataset.field] = field.type === 'checkbox' ? field.checked : field.value;
      card.updatedAt = nowIso();
      const read = node.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-read');
      if (read) {
        read.querySelector('strong').textContent = card.fact.trim() || '인지할 사실을 기록해 주세요.';
        read.querySelector('span').textContent = (card.status === 'mistaken' && card.actualFact.trim() ? '실제 사실: ' + card.actualFact.trim() + ' · ' : '') + (card.evidence.trim() || '확인 근거 미기록');
      }
      const prev = saveTimers.get(card.id);
      if (prev) clearTimeout(prev);
      if (immediate) { saveTimers.delete(card.id); void persist(); }
      else saveTimers.set(card.id, setTimeout(() => { saveTimers.delete(card.id); void persist(); }, 450));
    };
    panel.addEventListener('input', event => {
      if (event.target.matches('[data-field="person"],[data-field="fact"],[data-field="actualFact"],[data-field="evidence"],[data-field="hiddenFrom"],[data-field="concealmentNote"]')) editCard(event.target);
    });
    panel.addEventListener('change', event => {
      if (event.target.id === 'rpcm-knowledge-enabled') {
        room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled = event.target.checked;
        room.personStateInjectionEnabled = event.target.checked;
        // 사용자가 명시적으로 주입을 켤 때 간편 주입 관리의 일시 제외도 해제합니다.
        if (room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled && room.pending) room.pending.quickRemovedItems = quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).filter(item => !isKnowledgePendingItem(item));
        void persist().then(() => renderModalIfOpen());
      } else if (event.target.matches('[data-field]')) {
        editCard(event.target, true);
        if (['inject','status','category'].includes(event.target.dataset.field)) setTimeout(() => renderModalIfOpen(), 0);
      }
    });
    panel.querySelector('#rpcm-knowledge-add').onclick = async () => {
      const card = { id:'kc_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9), person:'', fact:'', status:'unconfirmed', category:'auto', evidence:'', inject:false, updatedAt:nowIso() };
      room.knowledgeCards.push(card);
      state.personKnowledgeCategory = 'all';
      await saveRoom(room);
      renderModalIfOpen();
      const item = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-knowledge-card')].find(node => node.dataset.knowledgeId === card.id);
      if (item) {
        item.open = true;
        item.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-read').hidden = true;
        item.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-fields').hidden = false;
        item.querySelector('[data-field="person"]')?.focus();
      }
    };
    panel.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const categoryButton = event.target.closest('[data-knowledge-category]');
      if (categoryButton) {
        state.personKnowledgeCategory = categoryButton.dataset.knowledgeCategory || 'all';
        renderModalIfOpen();
        return;
      }
      if (event.target.matches('[data-action="edit"]')) {
        const node = event.target.closest('[data-knowledge-id]');
        const fields = node?.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-fields');
        const read = node?.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-read');
        if (!fields || !read) return;
        fields.hidden = false;
        read.hidden = true;
        node.open = true;
        fields.querySelector('[data-field="fact"]')?.focus();
        return;
      }
      if (!event.target.matches('[data-action="delete"]')) return;
      const node = event.target.closest('[data-knowledge-id]');
      const index = cards.findIndex(card => card.id === node?.dataset.knowledgeId);
      if (index < 0 || !confirm('이 인지 카드를 삭제할까요?')) return;
      room.knowledgeCards = room.knowledgeCards.filter(card => card.id !== cards[index].id);
      void persist().then(() => renderModalIfOpen());
    });
  }

  // Core 1.0.6 cognition view adapter. It reads Wish cards without normalizing or
  // writing them; missing actor × fact states exist only in this ViewModel.
  // Cache counts only, reusing RP history fetched by the existing update range check.
  const personAppearanceCounts=new Map();
  function personCollectAppearances(room,turns){
    const vm=personCoreViewModel(room),counts=new Map();
    const matchers=vm.actors.map(actor=>({key:manualPersonName(actor.name),patterns:[actor.name,...actor.aliases].filter(name=>Array.from(name).length>=2).map(name=>{
      const escaped=name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');
      return new RegExp(/[a-z]/i.test(name)?`(?<![a-z0-9])${escaped}(?![a-z0-9])`:escaped,'i');
    })}));
    for(const turn of turns){const text=String(turn.text||[turn.userText,turn.assistantText].filter(Boolean).join('\n'));for(const actor of matchers)if(actor.patterns.some(pattern=>pattern.test(text)))counts.set(actor.key,(counts.get(actor.key)||0)+1);}
    personAppearanceCounts.set(String(room.chatId),counts);
  }
  function personCompareActors(room,a,b){
    const count=personAppearanceCounts.get(String(room.chatId));
    const fallback=actor=>(room.personStateRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.active!==false&&(manualPersonName(row.person)===manualPersonName(actor.name)||manualPersonName(row.target)===manualPersonName(actor.name))).length+(room.knowledgeCards||[]).filter(row=>!row.archived&&manualPersonName(row.person)===manualPersonName(actor.name)).length;
    return Number(b.pinned)-Number(a.pinned)||Number(b.pc)-Number(a.pc)
      ||(count?(count.get(manualPersonName(b.name))||0)-(count.get(manualPersonName(a.name))||0):fallback(b)-fallback(a))||a.catalogIndex-b.catalogIndex;
  }
  function personPinButton(room,name){
    const actor=personCoreViewModel(room).actors.find(row=>manualPersonName(row.name)===manualPersonName(name)),pinned=!!actor?.pinned;
    return `<button type="button" class="rpcm-person-pin ${pinned?'is-pinned':''}" data-person-pin="${esc(name)}" aria-pressed="${pinned}" aria-label="${esc(name)} ${pinned?'상단 고정 해제':'상단에 고정'}" title="${pinned?'고정 해제':'상단에 고정'}"><span class="rpcm-person-pin-mark" aria-hidden="true">📍</span><span class="rpcm-person-pin-label">${pinned?'고정됨':'고정'}</span></button>`;
  }
  async function personTogglePin(room,overlay,name){
    if(state.currentRoom!==room)return;
    const before=structuredClone(room.personUiActors||[]),actor=personCoreViewModel(room).actors.find(row=>manualPersonName(row.name)===manualPersonName(name));if(!actor)return;
    room.personUiActors ||= [];
    let entry=room.personUiActors.find(row=>manualPersonName(row.name)===manualPersonName(name));
    if(!entry){entry={name:actor.name,aliases:[...actor.aliases]};room.personUiActors.push(entry);}
    entry.pinned=!actor.pinned;
    try{await saveRoom(room);if(state.currentRoom===room){state.personCoreRailScroll=0;overlay.querySelector('.m3-people')?.scrollTo({left:0});renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);}}
    catch(error){room.personUiActors=before;notify('인물 고정을 저장하지 못했습니다: '+error.message,'error');}
  }

  function personCoreViewModel(room) {
    const norm = value => String(value || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
    const deletedNames=new Set((room.personDeletedNames||[]).map(norm));
    const internalId = value => /^[a-z][a-z0-9]*(?:_[a-z0-9]+)+$/i.test(String(value || '').trim());
    const metadata = Array.isArray(room.personUiActors) ? room.personUiActors.filter(row => row && String(row.name || '').trim()) : [];
    const actors = [], byName = new Map();
    const add = (name, details = {}) => {
      const label = String(name || '').trim(), key = norm(label);
      if (!key||deletedNames.has(key)) return null;
      let actor = byName.get(key);
      if (!actor) {
        actor = { name:label, aliases:[], pinned:false, pc:false, catalogIndex:actors.length };
        actors.push(actor); byName.set(key, actor);
      }
      if (Array.isArray(details.aliases) && details.aliases.length && !actor.aliases.length) actor.aliases = details.aliases.map(String).map(value => value.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      if (details.pinned === true) actor.pinned = true;
      return actor;
    };
    // Character slots are Wish's explicit canonical actor order. UI-only
    // actors follow them; pinned actors move ahead without inferring importance.
    (room.slots || []).filter(slot => slot.group === 'character').forEach(slot => add(slot.title, { aliases:slot.aliases }));
    metadata.forEach(row => add(row.name, row));
    const knowledge = (Array.isArray(room.knowledgeCards) ? room.knowledgeCards : []).filter(card => card && card.archived !== true);
    const records = (Array.isArray(room.personStateRecords) ? room.personStateRecords : []).filter(record => record && record.active !== false && ['speech','relationship'].includes(record.type));
    knowledge.forEach(card => {add(card.person);if(Array.isArray(card.hiddenFrom))card.hiddenFrom.forEach(add);if(Array.isArray(card.concealments))card.concealments.forEach(row=>{add(row?.holder);add(row?.target);});});
    records.forEach(record => { add(record.person); add(record.target); });
    // Match Wish's existing PC fallback without calling the normalizers: this
    // adapter must not write to the room while merely drawing Core's rail.
    let player = String(room.personPlayerName || '').trim();
    if (!player) {
      const counts = new Map();
      for (const record of records) {
        const target = String(record.target || '').trim();
        if (target && norm(target) !== norm(record.person)) counts.set(target, (counts.get(target) || 0) + 1);
      }
      const best = [...counts].sort((a,b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
      if (best?.[1] >= 2) player = best[0];
      else if (knowledge.some(card => String(card.person || '').trim() === 'USER') && actors.length > 1) player = 'USER';
    }
    if (player) { const actor=add(player); if(actor)actor.pc=true; }
    actors.sort((a,b)=>personCompareActors(room,a,b));

    const facts = [], byKey = new Map(), byText = new Map();
    for (const card of knowledge) {
      const text = String(card.fact || '').trim();
      if (!text) continue;
      const key = norm(card.key), textKey = norm(text).replace(/\s+/g,' ');
      let fact = (key && byKey.get(key)) || byText.get(textKey);
      if (!fact) {
        const explicit = [card.label,card.title].map(value => String(value || '').trim()).find(value => value && !internalId(value)) || '';
        const lines = text.split(/\n/);
        const firstLine = lines.map(value => value.trim()).find(value => value && !internalId(value)) || '';
        const readable = explicit || firstLine || '제목 없는 인지 정보';
        const title = readable.length <= 100 ? readable : readable.slice(0, 97).trimEnd() + '…';
        const displayContent = internalId(lines[0]) ? lines.slice(1).join('\n').trim() : text;
        fact = { id:String(card.id || facts.length), title, content:displayContent || '내용을 확인해 주세요.', cards:[], byActor:new Map(), concealments:[] };
        facts.push(fact);
      }
      if (key) byKey.set(key,fact);
      byText.set(textKey,fact);
      fact.cards.push(card);
      const actorKey = norm(card.person);
      if (!fact.byActor.has(actorKey)) fact.byActor.set(actorKey,[]);
      fact.byActor.get(actorKey).push(card);
      // Only explicitly stored concealment relations may appear here. Wish's
      // evidence prose is never interpreted as a new hidden relationship.
      const explicit = Array.isArray(card.concealments) ? card.concealments : [];
      explicit.forEach(row => {
        if (!row || !String(row.holder || '').trim() || !String(row.target || '').trim()) return;
        fact.concealments.push({ holder:String(row.holder), target:String(row.target), scope:String(row.scope || ''), publicVersion:String(row.publicVersion || '') });
      });
      if (card.status === 'knows' && Array.isArray(card.hiddenFrom) && String(card.person || '').trim()) card.hiddenFrom.forEach(target => {
        if (String(target || '').trim()) fact.concealments.push({ holder:String(card.person),target:String(target),scope:String(card.concealmentNote || ''),publicVersion:'' });
      });
    }
    const statusFor = (fact, actor) => {
      const cards = fact.byActor.get(norm(actor.name)) || [];
      if (!cards.length) return { kind:'unverified', label:'확인 안 됨', cardIds:[] };
      const raw = [...new Set(cards.map(card => String(card.status || '').toLowerCase()))];
      if (raw.length > 1) return { kind:'conflict', label:'상태 충돌 · 확인 필요', cardIds:cards.map(card => String(card.id || '')) };
      const labels = { knows:['aware','알고 있음'], aware:['aware','알고 있음'], 'does-not-know':['unaware','모름'], unaware:['unaware','모름'], unconfirmed:['unverified','확인 안 됨'], unverified:['unverified','확인 안 됨'], mistaken:['mistaken','잘못 알고 있음'], suspects:['suspects','의심'], private:['private','비공개'] };
      const [kind,label] = labels[raw[0]] || ['unverified','확인 안 됨'];
      return { kind,label,cardIds:cards.map(card => String(card.id || '')) };
    };
    const archived = [
      ...(Array.isArray(room.knowledgeCards) ? room.knowledgeCards : []).filter(card => card?.archived === true).map(card => ({ id:String(card.id || ''), kind:'knowledge', title:internalId(card.fact) ? '제목 없는 인지 정보' : String(card.fact || card.person || ''), evidence:String(card.evidence || ''), person:String(card.person || ''), at:String(card.updatedAt || '') })),
      ...(Array.isArray(room.personStateRecords) ? room.personStateRecords : []).filter(record => record?.active === false).map(record => ({ id:String(record.id || ''), kind:String(record.type || ''), title:`${record.person || ''} → ${record.target || ''}`, evidence:String(record.evidence || ''), person:String(record.person || ''), at:String(record.updatedAt || '') })),
    ];
    return { actors, facts, records, archived, statusFor };
  }

  function personCoreIcon(name) {
    const paths = {
      plus:'<path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14"/>',
      edit:'<path d="M15.4 4.6 19.4 8.6 8.6 19.4H4.6v-4Z"/>',
      check:'<path d="m5.4 12.6 4.4 4.4 8.8-9"/>',
      eye:'<path d="M2.6 12S6 5.8 12 5.8 21.4 12 21.4 12 18 18.2 12 18.2 2.6 12 2.6 12Z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.7"/>',
      chev:'<path d="m8 10.4 4 4 4-4"/>',
    };
    return `<svg class="ic ic-${name}" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">${paths[name] || ''}</svg>`;
  }
  function personCoreButton(label, action, arg = '', cls = 'mini') {
    const icon = action === 'addActor' || action === 'addFact' || action === 'addSpeech' || action === 'addRelationship' ? 'plus' : action === 'editFact' || action === 'editSpeech' || action === 'editRelationship' ? 'edit' : '';
    return `<button type="button" class="m3-btn ${cls}" data-person-core-action="${action}" data-person-core-arg="${esc(arg)}">${icon ? personCoreIcon(icon) : ''}<span>${esc(label)}</span></button>`;
  }

  // Keep Wish's original per-person detail renderer below Core's actor rail.
  // Render from a copy: opening a profile must never normalize or rewrite DB rows.
  function personReaderRelationshipLabel(record){
    const text=String(record.official||'').trim();
    return text&&!/[\n\r.!?。！？;:]/.test(text)&&text.split(/\s+/).length<=3&&Array.from(text).length<=18?text:'';
  }
  function personReaderTabs(pane,reviewCount){
    return `<nav class="rpcm-person-reader-tabs" aria-label="인물 상세 종류"><div role="tablist">${[['relationship','관계'],['knowledge','인지'],['speech','호칭']].map(([key,label])=>`<button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="${pane===key}" data-person-core-tab="${key}">${label}</button>`).join('')}</div><button type="button" data-person-core-tab="review" aria-pressed="${pane==='review'}">이전 검토 ${reviewCount}</button></nav>`;
  }
  function renderPersonProfileOverview(room,overlay,selected,pane='relationship'){
    const host=overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-person-profiles');if(!host)return;
    // Drawing a reader must not normalize, save, or send any request.
    const vm=personCoreViewModel(room),norm=manualPersonName,isActor=value=>norm(value)===norm(selected);
    const actor=vm.actors.find(row=>isActor(row.name))||{name:selected,aliases:[]};
    const records=vm.records.filter(row=>isActor(row.person)||isActor(row.target));
    const partners=new Map();
    for(const record of records){const name=isActor(record.person)?String(record.target||''):String(record.person||'');if(!name||isActor(name))continue;const key=norm(name);if(!partners.has(key))partners.set(key,{name,records:[]});partners.get(key).records.push(record);}
    const actorRank=new Map(vm.actors.map((actor,index)=>[norm(actor.name),index]));
    const ordered=[...partners.values()].sort((a,b)=>(actorRank.get(norm(a.name))??9999)-(actorRank.get(norm(b.name))??9999));
    const escapeValue=value=>esc(String(value||''));
    const edit=(kind,id)=>`<button type="button" class="rpcm-person-reader-edit" data-profile-edit="${kind}" data-profile-id="${escapeValue(id)}">편집</button>`;
    const fold=(label,value,cls='')=>value?`<details class="rpcm-person-reader-fold ${cls}"><summary>${label}</summary><p>${escapeValue(value)}</p></details>`:'';
    const field=(label,value)=>!String(value||'').trim()?'':String(value).length>240||String(value).split('\n').length>4?fold(label,value):`<div class="rpcm-person-reader-field"><small>${label}</small><p>${escapeValue(value)}</p></div>`;
    const recordHtml=(record,kind)=>`<section class="rpcm-person-reader-record" data-new-person-id="${escapeValue(record.id)}">${kind==='relationship'?field('현재 관계',record.official)+field('현재 태도',record.stance)+field('남은 쟁점',record.conflict)+fold('관계 변화',record.trajectory):field('호칭',record.address)+field('말투',record.tone)+field('적용 조건',record.context)}<div class="rpcm-person-reader-foot">${fold('확인 근거',record.evidence)}${edit(kind,record.id)}</div></section>`;
    const direction=(target,kind,outbound)=>{
      const from=outbound?selected:target.name,to=outbound?target.name:selected;
      const rows=target.records.filter(row=>row.type===kind&&(outbound?isActor(row.person):isActor(row.target)));
      return `<div class="rpcm-person-reader-direction"><h4>${escapeValue(from)} <span>→</span> ${escapeValue(to)}</h4>${rows.map(row=>recordHtml(row,kind)).join('')||'<p class="rpcm-person-reader-empty">이 방향의 저장된 기록이 없습니다.</p>'}</div>`;
    };
    const partnerHtml=(target,index,kind)=>{
      const relations=target.records.filter(row=>row.type===kind),preview=relations.find(row=>isActor(row.person))||relations[0];
      const label=kind==='relationship'?relations.map(personReaderRelationshipLabel).find(Boolean):'호칭·말투';
      const teaser=kind==='relationship'?preview?.stance||preview?.official:preview?.address||preview?.tone;
      const speech=kind==='relationship'&&target.records.some(row=>row.type==='speech');
      return `<details class="rpcm-person-reader-partner" data-person-partner="${escapeValue(target.name)}" ${index===0?'open':''}><summary>${personPinButton(room,target.name)}<span><strong>${escapeValue(target.name)}</strong>${label?`<em>${escapeValue(label)}</em>`:''}</span><p>${escapeValue(teaser||'저장된 양방향 기록을 확인하세요.')}</p></summary><div class="rpcm-person-reader-directions">${direction(target,kind,true)}${direction(target,kind,false)}</div>${speech?`<details class="rpcm-person-reader-speech"><summary>서로 쓰는 호칭·말투</summary><div class="rpcm-person-reader-directions">${direction(target,'speech',true)}${direction(target,'speech',false)}</div></details>`:''}</details>`;
    };
    const facts=vm.facts,knownFacts=facts.filter(fact=>fact.cards.some(card=>isActor(card.person)));
    const factHtml=fact=>{
      const status=vm.statusFor(fact,actor),own=fact.cards.filter(card=>isActor(card.person));
      const chips=vm.actors.map(person=>{const state=vm.statusFor(fact,person);return `<span class="rpcm-person-reader-status" data-status="${escapeValue(state.kind)}">${escapeValue(person.name)} · ${escapeValue(state.label)}</span>`;}).join('');
      const ownText=own.map(card=>`<section class="rpcm-person-reader-record">${field('이 인물이 인지한 범위',card.fact)}${card.status==='mistaken'?field('확인된 실제 사실',card.actualFact):''}<div class="rpcm-person-reader-foot">${fold('확인 근거',card.evidence)}${edit('knowledge',card.id)}</div></section>`).join('');
      const secrets=fact.concealments.map(row=>`<p class="rpcm-person-reader-secret"><b>${escapeValue(row.holder)} → ${escapeValue(row.target)}</b>에게 숨김${row.scope?` · ${escapeValue(row.scope)}`:''}${row.publicVersion?`<br>공개된 설명 · ${escapeValue(row.publicVersion)}`:''}</p>`).join('');
      return `<details class="rpcm-person-reader-fact" data-new-knowledge-ids="${escapeValue(JSON.stringify(fact.cards.map(card=>String(card.id))))}"><summary><strong>${escapeValue(fact.title)}</strong><span class="rpcm-person-reader-status" data-status="${escapeValue(status.kind)}">${escapeValue(status.label)}</span></summary><div class="rpcm-person-reader-fact-body">${ownText||'<p class="rpcm-person-reader-empty">이 인물의 인지 근거는 저장되지 않았습니다.</p>'}${secrets}<div class="rpcm-person-reader-gap"><small>같은 정보에 대한 인물별 인지</small><div>${chips}</div></div></div></details>`;
    };
    const archived=[...(room.knowledgeCards||[]).filter(row=>row.archived&&isActor(row.person)).map(row=>({row,kind:'knowledge',title:row.fact})),...(room.personStateRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.active===false&&(isActor(row.person)||isActor(row.target))).map(row=>({row,kind:row.type,title:`${row.person} → ${row.target}`}))];
    const reviewHtml=archived.map(({row,kind,title})=>`<article class="rpcm-person-reader-archived"><strong>${escapeValue(title)}</strong>${fold('확인 근거',row.evidence)}<button type="button" class="rpcm-person-reader-edit" data-profile-restore="${escapeValue(row.id)}" data-profile-kind="${kind}">다시 활성화</button></article>`).join('');
    const relational=ordered.filter(target=>target.records.some(row=>row.type==='relationship')),speaking=ordered.filter(target=>target.records.some(row=>row.type==='speech'));
    const active=activePendingItems(room.pending).some(item=>item.slotId==='person:'+encodeURIComponent(selected));
    const empty=text=>`<p class="rpcm-person-reader-empty">${text}</p>`;
    const body=pane==='knowledge'?`<div class="rpcm-person-reader-caption"><span>기록 있는 정보 ${knownFacts.length} · 전체 정보 ${facts.length}</span><small>이 인물의 카드가 없으면 확인 안 됨으로 표시합니다.</small></div>${facts.map(factHtml).join('')||empty('저장된 인지 정보가 없습니다.')}`:pane==='speech'?speaking.map((target,index)=>partnerHtml(target,index,'speech')).join('')||empty('저장된 호칭·말투가 없습니다.'):pane==='review'?reviewHtml||empty('이전에 종료한 기록이 없습니다.'):relational.map((target,index)=>partnerHtml(target,index,'relationship')).join('')||empty('저장된 관계 정보가 없습니다. 호칭 탭에서 서로 쓰는 호칭·말투를 확인할 수 있습니다.');
    host.dataset.pane=pane;host.dataset.personName=selected;
    host.innerHTML=`<section class="rpcm-person-reader" data-person-name="${escapeValue(selected)}"><header class="rpcm-person-reader-head"><span class="rpcm-person-reader-avatar">${escapeValue(Array.from(selected)[0]||'?')}</span><div class="rpcm-person-reader-head-copy"><h3>${escapeValue(selected)}</h3><p>${escapeValue(actor.aliases.join(' · '))}${actor.aliases.length?' · ':''}관계 ${relational.length} · 인지 ${knownFacts.length}</p></div><div class="rpcm-person-reader-head-actions">${active?'<span class="rpcm-person-reader-status" data-status="aware">지금 주입</span>':''}<details class="rpcm-person-reader-settings"><summary>주입 설정</summary><div><label><input id="rpcm-person-bundle-enabled" type="checkbox" ${room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled&&room.personStateInjectionEnabled?'checked':''}> 인물 세트 자동 주입</label></div></details></div></header>${personReaderTabs(pane,archived.length)}<div class="rpcm-person-reader-body" role="tabpanel" aria-label="${{relationship:'관계',knowledge:'인지',speech:'호칭·말투',review:'이전 검토'}[pane]}">${body}</div></section>`;
    const bundleInput=host.querySelector('#rpcm-person-bundle-enabled');
    if(bundleInput)bundleInput.onchange=async event=>{const enabled=event.target.checked;room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled=enabled;room.personStateInjectionEnabled=enabled;if(enabled&&room.pending)room.pending.quickRemovedItems=quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).filter(item=>!isKnowledgePendingItem(item));try{await saveRoom(room);await rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room);renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);}catch(error){notify('인물 세트 주입 갱신 실패: '+error.message,'error');}};
    host.onclick=event=>{
      const pin=event.target.closest('[data-person-pin]');if(pin){event.preventDefault();void personTogglePin(room,overlay,pin.dataset.personPin);return;}
      const edit=event.target.closest('[data-profile-edit]');if(edit){openPersonCoreCardEditor(room,overlay,edit.dataset.profileEdit,edit.dataset.profileId);return;}
      const restore=event.target.closest('[data-profile-restore]');if(!restore)return;
      const kind=restore.dataset.profileKind,id=restore.dataset.profileRestore;
      const row=(kind==='knowledge'?room.knowledgeCards:room.personStateRecords||[]).find(record=>String(record.id)===id);if(!row)return;
      if(kind==='knowledge')row.archived=false;else row.active=true;
      void saveRoom(room).then(()=>rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room)).then(()=>renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay)).catch(error=>notify('인물정보 복원 실패: '+error.message,'error',6000));
    };
    rpcmNewBadgeSchedule();
  }

  const relationshipMapNorm = value => String(value || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  function normalizeRelationshipGraph(raw,person,target){
    if(!raw||typeof raw!=='object'||Array.isArray(raw))return null;
    const kind=['family','hierarchy','peer','romance','other'].includes(raw.kind)?raw.kind:'other';
    const short=value=>{const text=String(value||'').trim();return text&&!/[\n\r.!?。！？;:]/.test(text)&&text.split(/\s+/).length<=3&&Array.from(text).length<=18?text:'';};
    const endpoint=value=>[person,target].find(name=>relationshipMapNorm(name)===relationshipMapNorm(value))||'';
    return {label:short(raw.label),kind,parent:kind==='family'?endpoint(raw.parent):'',superior:kind==='hierarchy'?endpoint(raw.superior):'',group:String(raw.group||'').trim().slice(0,60),members:[...new Set((Array.isArray(raw.members)?raw.members:[]).filter(x=>typeof x==='string').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean))].slice(0,50)};
  }

  function renderPersonCoreUi(room, overlay) {
    rpcmNewBadgeSchedule();
    const host = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-person-core'); if (!host) return;
    const roomKey = String(room.chatId || '');
    if (state.personCoreRailRoomId !== roomKey) {
      state.personCoreRailRoomId = roomKey; state.personCoreRailScroll = 0;
      try { state.personCoreSelectedActor = localStorage.getItem(`rpcm-person-selected:${roomKey}`) || ''; }
      catch (_) { state.personCoreSelectedActor = ''; }
    }
    const oldRail = host.querySelector('.m3-people')?.scrollLeft ?? state.personCoreRailScroll ?? 0;
    const oldDetail = host.querySelector('#rpcm-person-profiles');
    const previousDetailOpen = oldDetail?.dataset.personName === state.personCoreSelectedActor ? oldDetail.querySelector('.rpcm-profile')?.open ?? null : null;
    const vm = personCoreViewModel(room);
    const hiddenActors=new Set((room.personHiddenActors||[]).map(value=>String(value).trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')));
    const visibleActors=vm.actors.filter(actor=>!hiddenActors.has(actor.name.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')));
    const selected = visibleActors.some(actor => actor.name === state.personCoreSelectedActor) ? state.personCoreSelectedActor : '';
    state.personCoreSelectedActor = selected;
    const pane = ['knowledge','speech','relationship','review'].includes(state.personCorePane) ? state.personCorePane : 'relationship';
    state.personCorePane = pane;
    const badge = (text, variant = '') => `<span class="m3-kind ${variant}">${esc(text)}</span>`;
    const empty = text => `<div class="m3-empty">${esc(text)}</div>`;
    const factPanel = fact => {
      const included = fact.cards.filter(card => card.inject === true).length;
      const chips = vm.actors.map(actor => {
        const status = vm.statusFor(fact,actor);
        const cls = status.kind === 'aware' ? 'known' : status.kind === 'unaware' || status.kind === 'mistaken' || status.kind === 'private' || status.kind === 'conflict' ? 'never' : 'unknown';
        return `<span class="m3-chip ${cls}" title="${esc(status.kind === 'unverified' && !status.cardIds.length ? '이 인물의 상태는 저장되지 않았습니다.' : '')}">${status.kind === 'aware' ? personCoreIcon('check') : ''}${esc(actor.name)} · ${esc(status.label)}</span>`;
      }).join('');
      const concealments = fact.concealments.map(row => `<div class="m3-secret">${personCoreIcon('eye')}<span><b>${esc(row.holder)}</b>는 <b>${esc(row.target)}</b>에게 숨김${row.scope ? ` · ${esc(row.scope)}` : ''}${row.publicVersion ? ` · 공개용 “${esc(row.publicVersion)}”` : ''}</span></div>`).join('');
      const mistaken = fact.cards.filter(card => card.status === 'mistaken' && String(card.actualFact || '').trim()).map(card => `<div class="m3-secret">${personCoreIcon('eye')}<span><b>${esc(card.person)}</b>의 오해: ${esc(card.fact)}<br>실제 사실: ${esc(card.actualFact)}</span></div>`).join('');
