      return `<section class="m3-panel" data-key="ft-${esc(fact.id)}" data-new-knowledge-ids="${esc(JSON.stringify(fact.cards.map(card=>String(card.id))))}" style="--cc:var(--m3-accent)"><div class="m3-row">${badge('정보')}<b class="m3-grow">${esc(fact.title)}</b>${included ? badge(`주입 포함 ${included}개`,'is-on') : ''}</div><p>${esc(fact.content)}</p><div class="m3-know">${chips}</div>${concealments}${mistaken}<div class="m3-row m3-card-actions">${personCoreButton('편집','editFact',fact.id)}</div></section>`;
    };
    const speechPanel = record => `<section class="m3-panel m3-speech" data-key="sp-${esc(record.id)}" data-new-person-id="${esc(record.id)}" style="--cc:var(--m3-accent)"><div class="m3-row"><b class="m3-grow">${esc(record.person)}<span class="m3-arrow"> → </span>${esc(record.target)}</b>${record.inject ? badge('주입 포함') : badge('보관만')}</div><p class="m3-say">${record.address ? `“${esc(record.address)}”라고 부름` : '호칭 미확정 · 말투만 기록'}${record.tone ? `<small>${esc(record.tone)}</small>` : ''}${record.context ? `<small>${esc(record.context)}</small>` : ''}</p>${record.evidence ? `<details class="m3-fold2"><summary>확인 근거 ${personCoreIcon('chev')}</summary><div class="m3-fb">${esc(record.evidence)}</div></details>` : ''}<div class="m3-row m3-card-actions">${personCoreButton('편집','editSpeech',record.id)}</div></section>`;
    const relationPanel = record => `<section class="m3-panel" data-key="rel-${esc(record.id)}" data-new-person-id="${esc(record.id)}" style="--cc:var(--m3-accent)"><div class="m3-row"><b class="m3-grow">${esc(record.person)} → ${esc(record.target)}</b>${record.inject ? badge('주입 포함') : badge('보관만')}</div>${record.official ? `<p><b>공식 관계</b><br>${esc(record.official)}</p>` : ''}${record.stance ? `<p><b>현재 의미·태도</b><br>${esc(record.stance)}</p>` : ''}${record.conflict ? `<p><b>남은 쟁점</b><br>${esc(record.conflict)}</p>` : '<p class="m3-muted">기록된 남은 쟁점 없음</p>'}${record.trajectory ? `<details class="m3-fold2 rpcm-profile-trajectory"><summary>핵심 전환 · ${String(record.trajectory).length}자 ${personCoreIcon('chev')}</summary><div class="m3-fb">${esc(record.trajectory)}</div></details>` : ''}${record.evidence ? `<details class="m3-fold2"><summary>최근 대표 근거 ${personCoreIcon('chev')}</summary><div class="m3-fb">${esc(record.evidence)}</div></details>` : ''}<div class="m3-row m3-card-actions">${personCoreButton('편집','editRelationship',record.id)}</div></section>`;
    const reviewPanel = row => `<section class="m3-panel" data-key="rv-${esc(row.id)}" style="--cc:var(--m3-accent)"><div class="m3-row">${badge(row.kind === 'knowledge' ? '인지' : row.kind === 'speech' ? '호칭·말투' : '관계·감정선')}<b class="m3-grow">${esc(row.title)}</b></div>${row.evidence ? `<blockquote class="m3-quote">${esc(row.evidence)}</blockquote>` : ''}${row.at ? `<p class="m3-muted">마지막 기록 · ${esc(row.at)}</p>` : ''}<div class="m3-row m3-card-actions">${personCoreButton('다시 활성화','restore',`${row.kind}|${row.id}`)}</div></section>`;
    const tabHtml = selected?'':personReaderTabs(pane,vm.archived.length);
    const actorsHtml = `<div class="m3-sechead"><span class="m3-person-heading"><b>인물 ${visibleActors.length}${hiddenActors.size?` · 숨김 ${hiddenActors.size}`:''}</b><small class="m3-muted">고정 우선 · ${personAppearanceCounts.has(roomKey)?'RP 등장 턴 빈도순':'기록 빈도순 · RP 확인 후 자동 정렬'}</small></span><span class="m3-actions">${hiddenActors.size?personCoreButton(`숨긴 인물 ${hiddenActors.size}명`,'manageHidden'):''}${personCoreButton('전체 보기','selectActor','','mini' + (selected ? '' : ' is-on'))}${personCoreButton('인물 추가','addActor')}${personCoreButton(`인지 주입 ${room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`,'toggleInjection','knowledge',room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled ? 'mini is-on' : 'mini')}</span></div><div class="m3-people" role="list" aria-label="인물 선택">${visibleActors.map(actor => `<div class="m3-who${selected === actor.name ? ' is-selected' : ''}" data-key="who-${esc(actor.name)}" data-new-actor="${esc(actor.name)}" role="listitem">${actor.pc ? '<span class="m3-me">PC</span>' : ''}${personPinButton(room,actor.name)}<button type="button" class="m3-person-open" data-person-core-action="selectActor" data-person-core-arg="${esc(actor.name)}" aria-pressed="${selected === actor.name}"><span class="m3-av">${esc(Array.from(actor.name)[0] || '?')}</span><b>${esc(actor.name)}</b><small>${esc(actor.aliases.join(' · ') || '별칭 없음')}</small></button><button type="button" class="m3-person-meta" data-person-core-action="editActor" data-person-core-arg="${esc(actor.name)}" aria-label="${esc(actor.name)} 표시 정보 편집">${personCoreIcon('edit')}</button></div>`).join('') || '<p class="m3-muted">등록된 인물이 없습니다.</p>'}</div>`;
    const factsHtml = `<div class="m3-sechead"><span class="m3-title-help"><b>인지 현황</b><small class="m3-muted">정보 ${vm.facts.length}개 · 주입 포함 ${vm.facts.filter(fact => fact.cards.some(card => card.inject === true)).length}개</small></span>${personCoreButton('정보 추가','addFact')}</div><p class="m3-muted">노란 정보격차는 누가 누구에게 해당 사실을 실제로 숨기는지 기록한 내용입니다. ‘모름’ 상태만으로 은폐를 추정하지 않습니다. 정보 편집에서 숨기는 대상과 설명을 기록할 수 있습니다.</p>${vm.facts.map(factPanel).join('') || empty('인지 정보가 없습니다.')}`;
    const speech = vm.records.filter(record => record.type === 'speech');
    const relations = vm.records.filter(record => record.type === 'relationship');
    const content = selected ? '<div id="rpcm-person-profiles" class="rpcm-person-profiles"></div>'
      : pane === 'knowledge' ? factsHtml : pane === 'speech' ? `<div class="m3-toolbar"><span class="m3-muted m3-grow">현재 호칭·말투 ${speech.length}쌍</span><div class="m3-actions">${personCoreButton('추가','addSpeech')}${personCoreButton(`호칭·말투 주입 ${room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`,'toggleInjection','person',room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? 'mini is-on' : 'mini')}</div></div>${speech.map(speechPanel).join('') || empty('아직 호칭·말투 기록이 없습니다.')}` : pane === 'relationship' ? `<div class="m3-toolbar"><span class="m3-muted m3-grow">관계·감정선 ${relations.length}방향</span><div class="m3-actions">${personCoreButton('추가','addRelationship')}${personCoreButton(`관계 주입 ${room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`,'toggleInjection','person',room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? 'mini is-on' : 'mini')}</div></div>${relations.map(relationPanel).join('') || empty('아직 관계 기록이 없습니다.')}` : `<div class="m3-sechead"><span class="m3-muted">종료한 인지·호칭·관계 기록을 확인합니다.</span></div>${room.personLastImportSummary ? `<p class="m3-muted">최근 가져오기 · ${esc(room.personLastImportSummary)}</p>` : ''}${vm.archived.map(reviewPanel).join('') || empty('검토할 항목이 없습니다.')}`;
    host.innerHTML = `${actorsHtml}${tabHtml}<div class="m3-sub" data-key="cog-${pane}">${content}</div>`;
    host.querySelectorAll('.m3-who [data-person-pin]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>personTogglePin(room,overlay,button.dataset.personPin));
    if (selected) renderPersonProfileOverview(room,overlay,selected,pane,previousDetailOpen);
    const rail = host.querySelector('.m3-people');
    if (rail) { rail.scrollLeft = oldRail; rail.onscroll = () => { state.personCoreRailScroll = rail.scrollLeft; }; }
    host.querySelectorAll('[data-person-core-tab]').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => { state.personCorePane = button.dataset.personCoreTab; renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay); });
    host.querySelectorAll('[data-person-core-action]').forEach(button => button.onclick = async () => {
      const action = button.dataset.personCoreAction, arg = button.dataset.personCoreArg || '';
      if (action === 'selectActor') {
        state.personCoreSelectedActor = arg;
        try { localStorage.setItem(`rpcm-person-selected:${roomKey}`, arg); } catch (_) { /* UI preference only */ }
        renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'manageHidden') { openPersonHiddenDialog(room,overlay); return; }
      if (action === 'addActor' || action === 'editActor') { openPersonCoreActorDialog(room,overlay,action === 'editActor' ? arg : ''); return; }
      if (action === 'toggleInjection') {
        const enabled = arg === 'knowledge' ? !room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled : !room.personStateInjectionEnabled;
        room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled = enabled; room.personStateInjectionEnabled = enabled;
        if (enabled && room.pending) room.pending.quickRemovedItems = quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).filter(item => !isKnowledgePendingItem(item));
        try { await saveRoom(room); await rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room); renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay); }
        catch (error) { notify(`인물정보 주입 갱신 실패: ${error.message}`,'error',6000); }
        return;
      }
      if (action === 'restore') {
        const [kind,id] = arg.split('|');
        const card = kind === 'knowledge' ? (room.knowledgeCards || []).find(item => String(item.id) === id) : (room.personStateRecords || []).find(item => String(item.id) === id);
        if (!card) return;
        if (kind === 'knowledge') card.archived = false; else card.active = true;
        try { await saveRoom(room); await rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room); renderPersonPanel(room,overlay); }
        catch (error) { notify(`인물정보 복원 실패: ${error.message}`,'error',6000); }
        return;
      }
      const kind = action === 'addFact' || action === 'editFact' ? 'knowledge' : action === 'addSpeech' || action === 'editSpeech' ? 'speech' : 'relationship';
      const fact = kind === 'knowledge' ? vm.facts.find(row => row.id === arg) : null;
      openPersonCoreCardEditor(room,overlay,kind,action.startsWith('add') ? '' : fact ? fact.cards.map(card => String(card.id || '')) : arg);
    });
  }

  function openPersonCoreCardEditor(room,overlay,kind,id = '') {
    state.personAdvancedOpen = true;
    state.personPane = kind;
    const ids = Array.isArray(id) ? id.filter(Boolean) : id ? [id] : [];
    const source = kind === 'knowledge' ? (room.knowledgeCards || []).find(card => String(card.id) === ids[0]) : (room.personStateRecords || []).find(record => String(record.id) === ids[0]);
    // A Core fact card represents every actor's state. Keep all matching Wish
    // cards visible when its edit button opens the existing detailed editor.
    state.personFilter = ids.length > 1 ? '' : String(source?.person || '');
    const advanced = overlay.querySelector('.rpcm-person-advanced');
    if (!advanced) return;
    advanced.hidden = false; advanced.open = true;
    renderPersonPanel(room,overlay);
    if (!ids.length) {
      const add = kind === 'knowledge' ? overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-knowledge-add') : overlay.querySelector(`#rpcm-person-${kind} .rpcm-person-add`);
      add?.click();
      return;
    }
    const items = kind === 'knowledge' ? [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-knowledge-card')].filter(node => ids.includes(node.dataset.knowledgeId)) : [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-person-record')].filter(node => ids.includes(node.dataset.personRecord));
    for (const item of items) {
      item.open = true;
      const fields = item.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-fields,.rpcm-person-fields');
      const read = item.querySelector('.rpcm-knowledge-read,.rpcm-person-read');
      if (fields) fields.hidden = false;
      if (read) read.hidden = true;
    }
    items[0]?.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' });
  }

  function openPersonDeleteConfirm(room,overlay,name,closeEditor){
    const player=currentPersonPlayerName(room);
    if(!name||name===player||personCoreViewModel(room).actors.find(actor=>actor.name===name)?.pc){
      notify('현재 PC 인물은 삭제할 수 없습니다.','warn');return;
    }
    const key=value=>String(value||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
    const same=value=>key(value)===key(name);
    const ownKnowledge=(room.knowledgeCards||[]).filter(card=>same(card.person)).length;
    const ownRecords=(room.personStateRecords||[]).filter(row=>same(row.person)).length;
    const incoming=(room.personStateRecords||[]).filter(row=>!same(row.person)&&same(row.target)).length;
    const profiles=(room.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='character'&&same(slot.title)).length;
    const old=document.querySelector('#rpcm-person-delete-dialog');old?.remove();
    const backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.id='rpcm-person-delete-dialog';
    backdrop.innerHTML=`<div class="rpcm-person-dialog-card m3-ui" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="인물 삭제 확인"><div class="m3-sheet"><header><div class="m3-t"><b>${esc(name)} · 인물 삭제</b><small>삭제 범위를 확인한 뒤 완료하세요.</small></div><button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-delete-close aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">×</button></header><div class="m3-dialog-body"><p>인물 표시·별칭과 캐릭터 설정 ${profiles}개, 해당 인물이 주체인 인지 ${ownKnowledge}개·호칭/관계 ${ownRecords}개를 삭제합니다.</p><p>다른 인물이 이 인물을 대상으로 가진 정보 ${incoming}개는 기본으로 유지합니다. 현재상태·날짜로그·타임라인·물품·자료집·장기기억의 본문도 유지합니다.</p><label class="m3-toggle"><span>다른 인물의 대상 카드 ${incoming}개도 함께 삭제</span><input type="checkbox" data-delete-incoming></label></div><footer><button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-delete-close>취소</button><button type="button" class="m3-btn primary" data-delete-confirm>삭제 완료</button></footer></div></div>`;
    const close=()=>backdrop.remove();
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-delete-close]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=close);
    backdrop.onclick=event=>{if(event.target===backdrop)close();};
    backdrop.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')close();};
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-delete-confirm]').onclick=async()=>{
      const button=backdrop.querySelector('[data-delete-confirm]');button.disabled=true;
      const cascade=backdrop.querySelector('[data-delete-incoming]').checked;
      const before=JSON.stringify(room),candidate=structuredClone(room);
      const removedIds=new Set((candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>slot.group==='character'&&same(slot.title)).map(slot=>String(slot.id)));
      candidate.slots=(candidate.slots||[]).filter(slot=>!(slot.group==='character'&&same(slot.title)));
      candidate.personUiActors=(candidate.personUiActors||[]).filter(actor=>!same(actor.name));
      candidate.personHiddenActors=(candidate.personHiddenActors||[]).filter(value=>!same(value));
      candidate.personDeletedNames=[...new Set([...(candidate.personDeletedNames||[]),name])];
      candidate.knowledgeCards=(candidate.knowledgeCards||[]).filter(card=>!same(card.person));
      candidate.personStateRecords=(candidate.personStateRecords||[]).filter(row=>!same(row.person)&&!(cascade&&same(row.target)));
      if(candidate.pending){
        const keep=item=>!removedIds.has(String(item.slotId||''))&&String(item.slotId||'')!==`person:${encodeURIComponent(name)}`;
        candidate.pending.items=(candidate.pending.items||[]).filter(keep);
        candidate.pending.quickRemovedItems=(candidate.pending.quickRemovedItems||[]).filter(keep);
      }
      try{
        if(JSON.stringify(room)!==before)throw new Error('인물 정보가 변경되었습니다. 삭제 범위를 다시 확인해 주세요.');
        await saveRoom(candidate);
        const stored=await readManualStoredRoom(room);
        const fields=data=>JSON.stringify([data.slots,data.personUiActors,data.personHiddenActors,data.personDeletedNames,data.knowledgeCards,data.personStateRecords]);
        if(!stored||fields(stored)!==fields(candidate))throw new Error('삭제 후 로컬 저장본 확인에 실패했습니다. 새로고침 후 확인해 주세요.');
        if(JSON.stringify(room)!==before)throw new Error('삭제 중 방 데이터가 바뀌었습니다. 새로고침 후 확인해 주세요.');
        rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,stored);state.personCoreSelectedActor='';state.personFilter='';
        close();closeEditor();renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);renderPersonPanel(room,overlay);
        if(room.pending)try{await syncPendingCarrier(room,'person-delete');}catch(error){notify(`인물은 로컬에서 삭제됐지만 현재 주입 갱신에 실패했습니다: ${error.message}`,'error');}
        notify(`${name} 인물 삭제를 저장했습니다.`,'success');
      }catch(error){notify(`인물 삭제 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');button.disabled=false;}
    };
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
  }

  function openPersonHiddenDialog(room,overlay){
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-person-hidden-dialog')?.remove();
    const hidden=(room.personHiddenActors||[]).filter(name=>personCoreViewModel(room).actors.some(actor=>actor.name===name));
    const backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.id='rpcm-person-hidden-dialog';
    backdrop.innerHTML=`<div class="rpcm-person-dialog-card m3-ui" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="숨긴 인물 관리"><div class="m3-sheet"><header><div class="m3-t"><b>숨긴 인물 관리</b><small>상단 인물 선택 카드만 숨겼습니다. 저장된 인지·관계·호칭과 주입은 유지됩니다.</small></div><button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-hidden-close aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">×</button></header><div class="m3-dialog-body">${hidden.map(name=>`<div class="m3-row"><strong class="m3-grow">${esc(name)}</strong><button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-hidden-show="${esc(name)}">다시 표시</button></div>`).join('')||'<p>숨긴 인물이 없습니다.</p>'}</div><footer><button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-hidden-all>모두 표시</button><button type="button" class="m3-btn primary" data-hidden-close>완료</button></footer></div></div>`;
    const close=()=>backdrop.remove();
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-hidden-close]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=close);
    backdrop.onclick=event=>{if(event.target===backdrop)close();};
    backdrop.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')close();};
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-hidden-show]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{
      const prior=[...(room.personHiddenActors||[])];
      room.personHiddenActors=prior.filter(value=>value!==button.dataset.hiddenShow);
      try{await saveRoom(room);close();renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);}
      catch(error){room.personHiddenActors=prior;notify(`인물 카드 표시 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
    });
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-hidden-all]').onclick=async()=>{
      const prior=[...(room.personHiddenActors||[])];room.personHiddenActors=[];
      try{await saveRoom(room);close();renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);}
      catch(error){room.personHiddenActors=prior;notify(`인물 카드 표시 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
    };
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
  }

  function openPersonCoreActorDialog(room,overlay,name = '') {
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-person-core-dialog')?.remove();
    const existing = personCoreViewModel(room).actors.find(actor => actor.name === name);
    const backdrop = document.createElement('div'); backdrop.id = 'rpcm-person-core-dialog';
    backdrop.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-person-dialog-card m3-ui" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="인물 ${existing ? '편집' : '추가'}"><div class="m3-sheet"><header><div class="m3-t"><b>인물 ${existing ? '편집' : '추가'}</b><small>인물 카드 표시 정보 · 인지·관계 원문은 변경하지 않습니다.</small></div><button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-core-close aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">×</button></header><div class="m3-dialog-body"><label class="m3-fld"><span>이름</span><input data-core-name value="${esc(existing?.name || '')}" ${existing ? 'readonly' : ''} placeholder="인물 이름"></label><label class="m3-fld"><span>별칭</span><input data-core-aliases value="${esc(existing?.aliases.join(', ') || '')}" placeholder="쉼표로 구분"></label><label class="m3-fld"><span>소속·모임</span><input data-core-group value="${esc((room.personUiActors||[]).find(row=>row.name===existing?.name)?.groupName||'')}" placeholder="명시된 팀·가족·조직·친구 모임"></label><label class="m3-toggle"><span>PC</span><input type="checkbox" data-core-pc ${existing?.pc ? 'checked' : ''}></label><label class="m3-toggle"><span>주요·고정 인물</span><input type="checkbox" data-core-pinned ${existing?.pinned ? 'checked' : ''}></label></div><footer>${existing?'<button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-core-hide>상단 카드 숨기기</button>':''}${existing&&!existing.pc?'<button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-core-delete>인물 삭제</button>':''}<button type="button" class="m3-btn" data-core-close>취소</button><button type="button" class="m3-btn primary" data-core-save>저장</button></footer></div></div>`;
    const close = () => backdrop.remove();
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-delete]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>openPersonDeleteConfirm(room,overlay,existing?.name||'',close));
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-hide]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
      if(!existing)return;
      const prior=[...(room.personHiddenActors||[])];
      room.personHiddenActors=[...new Set([...prior,existing.name])];
      try{await saveRoom(room);close();state.personCoreSelectedActor='';renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);notify(`${existing.name} 상단 카드만 숨겼습니다.`,'success');}
      catch(error){room.personHiddenActors=prior;notify(`인물 카드 숨기기 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
    });
    backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-core-close]').forEach(button => button.onclick = close);
    backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) close(); };
    backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') close(); };
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-save]').onclick = async () => {
      const actorName = String(backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-name]').value || '').trim();
      if (!actorName) { notify('인물 이름을 입력해 주세요.','warn'); return; }
      if (!existing && personCoreViewModel(room).actors.some(actor => actor.name.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR') === actorName.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR'))) { notify('같은 이름의 인물이 이미 있습니다.','warn'); return; }
      const aliases = [...new Set(String(backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-aliases]').value || '').split(',').map(value => value.trim()).filter(Boolean))];
      const groupName=String(backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-group]').value||'').trim();
      const pinned = backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-pinned]').checked;
      const pc = backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-pc]').checked;
      const prior = structuredClone(room.personUiActors || []), priorPlayer = room.personPlayerName, priorDeleted=structuredClone(room.personDeletedNames||[]);
      room.personUiActors = Array.isArray(room.personUiActors) ? room.personUiActors : [];
      const row = room.personUiActors.find(item => String(item.name || '').toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR') === actorName.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR'));
      if (row) { row.aliases = aliases; row.pinned = pinned; row.groupName=groupName; }
      else room.personUiActors.push({ name:actorName, aliases, pinned, groupName });
      room.personDeletedNames=(room.personDeletedNames||[]).filter(value=>value.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')!==actorName.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR'));
      if (pc) room.personPlayerName = actorName;
      else if (String(room.personPlayerName || '').toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR') === actorName.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR')) room.personPlayerName = '';
      try { await saveRoom(room); }
      catch (error) { room.personUiActors = prior; room.personPlayerName = priorPlayer; room.personDeletedNames=priorDeleted; notify(`인물 표시 정보 저장 실패: ${error.message}`,'error',6000); return; }
      close(); renderPersonCoreUi(room,overlay);
      if (room.pending && priorPlayer !== room.personPlayerName) {
        try { await rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room); }
        catch (error) { notify(`인물 표시 정보는 저장됐지만 주입 갱신에 실패했습니다: ${error.message}`,'error',6000); }
      }
    };
    document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-core-name]')?.focus();
  }

  function relationshipGraphFieldsHtml(record){
    const graph=normalizeRelationshipGraph(record.graph,record.person,record.target)||{kind:'',label:'',parent:'',superior:'',group:'',members:[]};
    return `<details class="rpcm-person-graph-editor"><summary>관계도 표시 설정</summary><p>부모·상관은 실제 인물을 지정하세요. 친구·연인과 인물 방향만으로 상하 관계를 만들지 않습니다.</p><label>짧은 관계명<input data-person-graph="label" maxlength="18" value="${esc(graph.label)}" placeholder="친구, 연인, 양부·양녀"></label><label>관계 형태<select data-person-graph="kind">${[['','기존 관계 기록에서 표시'],['family','가족'],['hierarchy','지휘·고용 관계'],['peer','친구·동료·협력'],['romance','연인·배우자'],['other','기타']].map(([value,label])=>`<option value="${value}" ${graph.kind===value?'selected':''}>${label}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>부모 또는 상관<select data-person-graph="anchor"><option value="">해당 없음·미확인</option>${[record.person,record.target].map(name=>`<option value="${esc(name)}" ${(graph.parent||graph.superior)===name?'selected':''}>${esc(name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>명시된 소속·모임<input data-person-graph="group" value="${esc(graph.group)}"></label><label>확인된 구성원<input data-person-graph="members" value="${esc(graph.members.join(', '))}" placeholder="쉼표로 구분 · 실제 소속 인물만"></label></details>`;
  }

  function renderPersonPanel(room, overlay) {
    rpcmNewBadgeSchedule();
    const section = overlay.querySelector('#rpcm-section-people');
    if (!section) return;
    const records = normalizePersonStateRecords(room);
    const knowledge = normalizeKnowledgeCards(room);
    const names = [...new Set([...knowledge.filter(card => !card.archived).map(card => card.person.trim()), ...records.filter(record => record.active).map(record => record.person.trim())].filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b,'ko'));
    const pane = ['knowledge','speech','relationship','review'].includes(state.personPane) ? state.personPane : 'knowledge';
    state.personPane = pane;
    const filter = names.includes(state.personFilter) ? state.personFilter : '';
    const editContext = section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-edit-context');
    const returnSource = state.personReturnSource?.chatId === String(room.chatId) ? state.personReturnSource : null;
    if (editContext) {
      editContext.hidden = !returnSource;
      if (returnSource) {
        const kind = { knowledge:'인지', speech:'호칭·말투', relationship:'관계·감정선', review:'이전 검토' }[pane] || '인물정보';
        editContext.querySelector('strong').textContent = `${filter || returnSource.name || '인물'} · ${kind} 편집 중`;
        editContext.querySelector('button').onclick = () => {
          const record = returnSource.kind === 'knowledge'
            ? (room.knowledgeCards || []).find(item => String(item.id) === returnSource.id)
            : (room.personStateRecords || []).find(item => String(item.id) === returnSource.id);
          const name = String(record?.person || returnSource.name || '').trim();
          state.personReturnSource = null;
          state.personAdvancedOpen = false;
          const advanced = section.querySelector('.rpcm-person-advanced');
          advanced.open = false; advanced.hidden = true;
          renderPersonCoreUi(room, overlay);
          const actor = [...section.querySelectorAll('.m3-who')].find(node => node.querySelector('[data-person-core-arg]')?.dataset.personCoreArg === name);
          actor?.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' });
          editContext.hidden = true;
        };
      }
    }
    const applyFilter = () => {
      section.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-knowledge-panel .rpcm-knowledge-card').forEach(card => {
        const match = knowledge.find(item => item.id === card.dataset.knowledgeId);
        card.hidden = !!filter && match?.person.trim() !== filter;
      });
      section.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-person-group').forEach(group => { group.hidden = !!filter && group.dataset.person !== filter; });
    };
    section.querySelectorAll('[data-person-pane]').forEach(button => {
      const active = button.dataset.personPane === pane;
      button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(active));
      button.tabIndex = active ? 0 : -1;
      button.onclick = () => { state.personPane = button.dataset.personPane; renderPersonPanel(room, overlay); };
    });
    section.querySelectorAll('[data-person-content]').forEach(node => { node.hidden = node.dataset.personContent !== pane; });
    let picker = section.querySelector('.rpcm-person-filter');
    if (!picker) {
      picker = document.createElement('label');
      picker.className = 'rpcm-person-filter';
      picker.innerHTML = '<span>인물</span><select id="rpcm-person-filter"></select>';
      section.querySelector('.rpcm-person-tabs').after(picker);
    }
    picker.querySelector('select').innerHTML = '<option value="">전체 인물</option>' + names.map(name => `<option value="${esc(name)}"${filter === name ? ' selected' : ''}>${esc(name)}</option>`).join('');
    picker.querySelector('select').onchange = event => { state.personFilter = event.target.value; renderPersonPanel(room, overlay); };

    const label = type => type === 'speech' ? '호칭·말투' : '관계·감정선';
    for (const type of ['speech','relationship']) {
      const parent = section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-' + type);
      const active = records.filter(record => record.type === type && record.active);
      const grouped = new Map();
      for (const record of active) {
        const name = record.person.trim() || '이름 미입력';
        if (!grouped.has(name)) grouped.set(name, []);
        grouped.get(name).push(record);
      }
      const editing = new Set([...parent.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-person-fields:not([hidden])')].map(node => node.closest('[data-person-record]')?.dataset.personRecord));
      parent.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-person-type-head"><div><strong>${label(type)} · ${active.length}개</strong><span>${type === 'speech' ? '누가 누구를 어떻게 부르는지, 상황별 말투를 구분합니다.' : '공식 관계와 각 인물의 감정·경계를 방향별로 구분합니다.'}</span></div><label><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-person-injection" ${room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? 'checked' : ''}> 호칭·관계 주입 ${room.personStateInjectionEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}</label><button type="button" class="rpcm-add-btn rpcm-person-add">＋ 카드</button></div>`
        + ([...grouped].map(([name, entries]) => `<div class="rpcm-person-group" data-person="${esc(name)}"><h3>👤 ${esc(name)} <small>${entries.length}개</small></h3>${entries.map(record => {
          const detail = record.type === 'speech' ? [record.address && '호칭 ' + record.address, record.tone && '말투 ' + record.tone, record.context && '조건 ' + record.context].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') : [record.official && '공식 ' + record.official, record.stance && '태도 ' + record.stance, record.conflict && '갈등 ' + record.conflict, record.trajectory && `핵심 전환 ${record.trajectory.length}자`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
          const fields = record.type === 'speech' ? `<label>호칭<input data-person-field="address" value="${esc(record.address)}"></label><label>말투<input data-person-field="tone" value="${esc(record.tone)}"></label><label>적용 조건<input data-person-field="context" value="${esc(record.context)}"></label>` : `<label>공식 관계<input data-person-field="official" value="${esc(record.official)}"></label><label>이 인물의 태도<textarea data-person-field="stance" rows="2">${esc(record.stance)}</textarea></label><label>남은 갈등·경계<textarea data-person-field="conflict" rows="2">${esc(record.conflict)}</textarea></label><label>핵심 전환<textarea data-person-field="trajectory" rows="3">${esc(record.trajectory || '')}</textarea></label>`;
          return `<details class="rpcm-person-record" data-person-record="${esc(record.id)}"><summary><strong>${esc(record.person || '인물')} → ${esc(record.target || '상대')}</strong><span>${esc(detail || '내용 입력')}</span><em>${record.inject ? '주입 포함' : '보관만'}</em></summary><div class="rpcm-person-read"><p>${esc(detail || '내용 입력')}</p><small>${esc(record.evidence || '근거 미기록')}</small><button type="button" data-person-action="edit">편집</button></div><div class="rpcm-person-fields" hidden><label>인물<input data-person-field="person" value="${esc(record.person)}"></label><label>상대<input data-person-field="target" value="${esc(record.target)}"></label>${fields}${record.type==='relationship'?relationshipGraphFieldsHtml(record):''}<label>확인 근거<textarea data-person-field="evidence" rows="2">${esc(record.evidence)}</textarea></label><div class="rpcm-person-record-actions"><label><input data-person-field="inject" type="checkbox" ${record.inject ? 'checked' : ''}> 주입에 포함</label><button type="button" data-person-action="archive">종료 · 보관</button><button type="button" data-person-action="close">접기</button></div></div></details>`;
        }).join('')}</div>`).join('') || '<div class="rpcm-person-empty">등록된 카드가 없습니다. 전용 방 결과물을 가져오거나 직접 추가하세요.</div>');
      for (const node of parent.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-person-record')) {
        if (editing.has(node.dataset.personRecord)) { node.open = true; node.querySelector('.rpcm-person-fields').hidden = false; node.querySelector('.rpcm-person-read').hidden = true; }
      }
      parent.querySelector('.rpcm-person-injection').onchange = async event => {
        room.personStateInjectionEnabled = event.target.checked;
        room.knowledgeInjectionEnabled = event.target.checked;
        if (room.personStateInjectionEnabled && room.pending) room.pending.quickRemovedItems = quickRemovedPendingItems(room.pending).filter(item => !isKnowledgePendingItem(item));
        await saveRoom(room);
        try { await rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room); } catch (error) { notify(`인물정보 주입 갱신 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 5400); }
        renderModalIfOpen();
      };
      parent.querySelector('.rpcm-person-add').onclick = async () => {
        const record = {id:'ps_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8),key:'',type,person:filter,target:'',address:'',tone:'',context:'',official:'',stance:'',conflict:'',trajectory:'',evidence:'',active:true,inject:false};
        room.personStateRecords.push(record);
        await saveRoom(room);
        renderModalIfOpen();
        const card = [...overlay.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-person-record')].find(node => node.dataset.personRecord === record.id);
        if (card) { card.open = true; card.querySelector('.rpcm-person-fields').hidden = false; card.querySelector('.rpcm-person-read').hidden = true; card.querySelector('[data-person-field="person"]')?.focus(); }
      };
      parent.onclick = event => {
        const action = event.target.closest('[data-person-action]');
        if (!action) return;
        const node = action.closest('.rpcm-person-record');
        const record = records.find(item => item.id === node?.dataset.personRecord);
        if (!record) return;
        if (action.dataset.personAction === 'edit' || action.dataset.personAction === 'close') {
          const show = action.dataset.personAction === 'edit';
          node.querySelector('.rpcm-person-read').hidden = show;
          node.querySelector('.rpcm-person-fields').hidden = !show;
          if (show) node.querySelector('[data-person-field="person"]')?.focus();
        } else if (action.dataset.personAction === 'archive' && confirm('이 카드를 주입에서 제외하고 이전 검토로 보낼까요?')) {
          record.active = false; record.inject = false;
          void saveRoom(room).then(() => rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room)).then(() => renderModalIfOpen()).catch(error => notify(error.message,'error'));
        }
      };
      const update = event => {
        const field = event.target.closest('[data-person-field],[data-person-graph]');
        const record = records.find(item => item.id === field?.closest('.rpcm-person-record')?.dataset.personRecord);
        if (!record) return;
        if(field.hasAttribute('data-person-graph')){const card=field.closest('.rpcm-person-record'),value=name=>card.querySelector(`[data-person-graph="${name}"]`)?.value||'',chosen=value('kind'),kind=chosen||((field.dataset.personGraph!=='kind'&&(value('label')||value('group')||value('members')))?'other':''),anchor=value('anchor');if(kind&&!chosen)card.querySelector('[data-person-graph="kind"]').value=kind;record.graph=kind?normalizeRelationshipGraph({label:value('label'),kind,parent:kind==='family'?anchor:'',superior:kind==='hierarchy'?anchor:'',group:value('group'),members:value('members').split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean)},record.person,record.target):null;}
        else record[field.dataset.personField] = field.type === 'checkbox' ? field.checked : field.value;
        queueRoomAutoSave(room, 500);
        clearTimeout(section._rpcmPersonSync);
        const syncPerson = () => {
          if (rpcmHasActiveComposition()) { section._rpcmPersonSync = setTimeout(syncPerson, 200); return; }
          void rebuildPendingKnowledgeItem(room).catch(error => notify(`인물정보 주입 갱신 실패: ${error.message}`,'error'));
        };
        section._rpcmPersonSync = setTimeout(syncPerson, 850);
      };
      parent.oninput = update;
      parent.onchange = event => { if (event.target.matches('[data-person-field],[data-person-graph]')) { update(event); if (event.target.dataset.personField === 'person') state.personFilter = event.target.value.trim(); renderPersonPanel(room, overlay); state.capacityUiRefresh?.(); } };
    }

    const review = section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-review');
    const previous = [...knowledge.filter(card => card.archived).map(card => ({id:card.id,title:`${card.person} · ${card.fact}`,kind:'knowledge'})), ...records.filter(record => !record.active).map(record => ({id:record.id,title:`${record.person} → ${record.target} · ${label(record.type)}`,kind:record.type}))];
    review.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-person-type-head"><div><strong>이전 검토 · ${previous.length}개</strong><span>종료한 항목을 보관합니다. 다시 활성화할 수 있습니다.</span></div></div>` + (previous.map(item => `<div class="rpcm-person-archive-row"><span>${esc(item.title)}</span><button type="button" data-person-restore="${esc(item.id)}" data-person-kind="${esc(item.kind)}">다시 활성화</button></div>`).join('') || '<div class="rpcm-person-empty">보관된 항목이 없습니다.</div>') + (room.personLastImportSummary ? `<div class="rpcm-person-last-import">최근 가져오기 · ${esc(room.personLastImportSummary)}</div>` : '');
    review.onclick = event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('[data-person-restore]');
      if (!button) return;
      if (button.dataset.personKind === 'knowledge') { const card = knowledge.find(item => item.id === button.dataset.personRestore); if (card) card.archived = false; }
      else { const record = records.find(item => item.id === button.dataset.personRestore); if (record) record.active = true; }
      void saveRoom(room).then(() => renderModalIfOpen());
    };
    applyFilter();

    const importBox = section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-import');
    const closeImport=()=>{state.personImportDraft=importBox.querySelector('textarea').value;importBox.hidden=true;state.personImportOpen=false;section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-import-toggle').focus({preventScroll:true});};
    importBox.querySelectorAll('[data-person-import-close]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=closeImport);
    importBox.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();closeImport();}};
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-import-toggle').onclick = () => { importBox.hidden = !importBox.hidden; state.personImportOpen = !importBox.hidden; if (!importBox.hidden) importBox.querySelector('textarea').focus(); };
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-export').onclick = async () => {
      const copied = await copyPlainText(personStateExportText(room));
      notify(copied ? '기존 인물정보를 복사했습니다. 인물 전용 방의 다음 요청에 함께 넣어 주세요.' : '복사에 실패했습니다.', copied ? 'success' : 'error', 4300);
    };
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-guide-copy').onclick = async () => {
      const copied = await copyPlainText(effectivePersonGuide(room));
      notify(copied ? '인물정보 지침을 복사했습니다. 전용 방에 한 번 넣어 주세요.' : '인물정보 지침 복사에 실패했습니다.', copied ? 'success' : 'error', 4300);
    };
    const guidePanel=section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-guide-panel');
    const guideText=section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-guide-text');
    const guideModified=section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-guide-modified');
    if (guidePanel && guideText) {
      guideText.value=effectivePersonGuide(room);
      const refresh=()=>{ guideModified.textContent=guideText.value===canonicalGuideText('personInfo')?'기본':'수정됨'; };
      refresh(); guideText.oninput=refresh;
      section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-guide-toggle').onclick=()=>{guidePanel.hidden=!guidePanel.hidden;if(!guidePanel.hidden) guideText.focus();};
      section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-guide-save').onclick=async()=>{room.personStateGuide=guideText.value;await saveRoom(room);refresh();notify('인물정보 지침을 저장했습니다.','success');};
      section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-guide-reset').onclick=async()=>{if(!confirm('인물정보 지침을 기본값으로 되돌릴까요?'))return;guideText.value=canonicalGuideText('personInfo');room.personStateGuide=guideText.value;await saveRoom(room);refresh();};
    }
    const fileInput = section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-import-file');
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-import-file-button').onclick = () => fileInput.click();
    fileInput.onchange = async () => {
      const file = fileInput.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      if (file.size > 600000) { notify('인물 결과 TXT가 너무 큽니다. 나누어 가져와 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
      importBox.querySelector('textarea').value = await file.text();
      state.personImportDraft = importBox.querySelector('textarea').value;
      fileInput.value = '';
      section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-preview').click();
    };
    section.querySelector('#rpcm-person-preview').onclick = () => openPersonStateImportPreview(room, importBox.querySelector('textarea').value);
    const editorClose = section.querySelector('.rpcm-person-core-editor-close');
    if (editorClose) editorClose.onclick = event => {
      event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
      state.personAdvancedOpen = false;
      const advanced = section.querySelector('.rpcm-person-advanced');
      advanced.open = false; advanced.hidden = true;
      state.personReturnSource = null;
      renderPersonCoreUi(room, overlay);
    };
    renderPersonCoreUi(room, overlay);
  }

  function openPersonStateImportPreview(room, text) {
    const {records, errors} = parsePersonStateOutput(text);
    const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
    backdrop.className = 'rpcm-person-preview-backdrop';
    backdrop.innerHTML = '<div class="rpcm-person-preview-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="인물정보 가져오기 검토"><header><strong>🫂 인물정보 분류 미리보기</strong><button type="button" data-person-preview-close aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></header><p>기존 정보는 그대로 두고 선택한 카드만 추가·갱신합니다. 종료에도 직접 근거가 필요합니다.</p><div class="rpcm-person-preview-list"></div><label class="rpcm-person-replace-option"><input type="checkbox" id="rpcm-person-archive-omitted"> 전체본에 없는 기존 항목을 이전 검토로 이동 <small>기존 기록은 삭제되지 않으며 되돌릴 수 있습니다.</small></label><footer><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-person-preview-close>취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" id="rpcm-person-apply">선택 항목 적용</button></footer></div>';
    const list = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-person-preview-list');
    if (errors.length) list.innerHTML = '<div class="rpcm-person-import-errors"><strong>형식 오류 · 적용할 수 없습니다</strong>' + errors.map(error => `<p>${esc(error)}</p>`).join('') + '</div>';
    else if (!records.length) list.innerHTML = '<div class="rpcm-person-empty">결과물에 현재 유효한 인물정보 카드가 없습니다.</div>';
    else list.innerHTML = records.map((entry,index) => {
      const old = personRecordMatch(room, entry);
      const collidedId = entry.id && [...normalizeKnowledgeCards(room), ...normalizePersonStateRecords(room)].some(item => item.id === entry.id && item !== old);
      const actorMismatch = !!old && (old.person.trim() !== entry.person || (entry.type !== 'knowledge' && old.target.trim() !== entry.target));
      const unknownEnd = entry.op === 'end' && !old;
      const blocked = actorMismatch || unknownEnd || collidedId;
      const title = entry.type === 'knowledge' ? `${entry.person} · ${entry.fact || old?.fact || entry.key}` : `${entry.person} → ${entry.target} · ${entry.type === 'speech' ? '호칭·말투' : '관계·감정선'}`;
      const value = entry.type === 'knowledge' ? `${entry.status} · ${entry.fact}` : entry.type === 'speech' ? `${entry.address} · ${entry.tone} · ${entry.context}` : `${entry.official} · ${entry.stance} · ${entry.conflict}${entry.trajectory ? ` · 핵심 전환 ${entry.trajectory.length}자` : ''}`;
      const beforeTrajectory = String(old?.trajectory || '');
      const afterTrajectory = entry.trajectoryPresent ? entry.trajectory : beforeTrajectory;
      const trajectoryDiff = entry.type === 'relationship' && entry.op === 'upsert' && beforeTrajectory !== afterTrajectory
